(2) Graduated replacement of 25% of Transformer attention layers gives the best perplexity in our matched-budget sweep (268.0 vs 282.4 pure Transformer). (3) Hub-GPT provides strictly causal routing, achieving PPL 211.5 +/- 0.4 over 3 seeds (post council-causal fix); approximately 3 PPL worse than Jamba's 208.5 +/- 0.7, a measurable quality cost for avoiding O(n^2) computation. Post-fix, chunk size C has little effect; the pre-fix chunk-size benefit was an artifact of a bidirectional-council leak we found in adversarial review.
  A multi-seed hub-count sweep (~105 runs across M=1-32) reveals M=8-14 as the reliably-converging sub-band (4-5/5 seeds); M=6 is rescued to 5/5 by orthogonal regularization, while M>=20 shows increasing seed sensitivity. Companion paper arXiv:2603.20997 (Basu, 2026) defines the routing diagnostic task. Code and scripts will be released.

</details>


### [36] [Deep Learning for Model Calibration in Simulation of Itaconic Acid Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22496)
*Daria Fokina,Marco Baldan,Constantin Romankiewicz,Wolfgang Laudensack,Roland Ulber,Michael Bortz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用深度学习方法（直接深度学习DDL和条件流匹配CFM）估算衣康酸生产过程的动力学参数，结果表明CFM比DDL和非线性回归更准确、更鲁棒，能够跨操作条件和规模可靠预测系统行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性回归方法在估算生物过程动力学参数时存在局限性，需要开发更灵活、数据高效的深度学习方法来实现跨操作条件和反应器规模的可靠参数估计。

Method: 采用两种深度学习策略（直接深度学习DDL和生成式条件流匹配CFM）进行动力学参数估计，以非线性回归为基准方法，基于不同搅拌速度和反应器规模的实际分批实验数据进行对比评估。

Result: CFM始终比DDL获得更准确的结果；CFM预测的浓度分布与非线性回归结果高度一致，而DDL偏差较大；在放大实验中，CFM的泛化能力和鲁棒性均优于DDL。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配（CFM）能够可靠预测不同操作条件和规模下的系统行为，为动态生物过程模型的参数估计提供了一种灵活且数据高效的框架。

Abstract: In this study, deep learning is used to estimate kinetic parameters for modeling itaconic acid production based on real batch experiments conducted at different agitation speeds and reactor scales. Two deep learning strategies, namely direct deep learning (DDL) and generative conditional flow matching (CFM) are compared and benchmarked against nonlinear regression as a reference method. Compared with DDL, CFM consistently yields more accurate results. The concentration profiles predicted by CFM closely match those obtained from nonlinear regression, whereas DDL results in larger deviations. Similar behavior is observed in the scale-up experiments, where the CFM model again generalizes better and is more robust than the direct approach. These findings demonstrate that CFM can reliably predict system behavior across different operating conditions and scales, offering a flexible and data-efficient framework for parameter estimation in dynamic bioprocess models.

</details>


### [37] [FeatEHR-LLM: Leveraging Large Language Models for Feature Engineering in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22534)
*Hojjat Karami,David Atienza,Jean-Philippe Thiran,Anisoara Ionescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FeatEHR-LLM是一个利用大语言模型从不规则采样的电子健康记录时间序列中生成临床上可解释表格特征的框架。LLM仅基于数据模式而非原始患者记录操作，通过工具增强生成机制处理不规则时序数据。在8项临床预测任务中，7项达到最高平均AUROC，最高提升6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子健康记录特征工程方法要么缺乏临床领域意识，要么假设数据是干净、规则采样的，无法适应真实EHR数据中不规则观测间隔、可变测量频率和结构性稀疏等问题。

Method: 采用大语言模型仅基于数据集模式和任务描述（而非原始患者记录）生成特征；通过工具增强生成机制（tool-augmented generation）配备专门处理不规则时序数据的例程，生成可执行的特征提取代码；支持单变量和多变量特征生成，并采用迭代式、验证在环（validation-in-the-loop）流水线。

Result: 在四个ICU数据集上的八项临床预测任务中，FeatEHR-LLM在7项任务上取得最高平均AUROC，相比强基线方法最高提升6个百分点。

Conclusion: FeatEHR-LLM通过结合LLM的临床语义理解与工具增强代码生成，有效解决了不规则采样EHR数据中的特征工程难题，在保护隐私的同时显著提升了临床预测性能。

Abstract: Feature engineering for Electronic Health Records (EHR) is complicated by irregular observation intervals, variable measurement frequencies, and structural sparsity inherent to clinical time series. Existing automated methods either lack clinical domain awareness or assume clean, regularly sampled inputs, limiting their applicability to real-world EHR data. We present \textbf{FeatEHR-LLM}, a framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate clinically meaningful tabular features from irregularly sampled EHR time series. To limit patient privacy exposure, the LLM operates exclusively on dataset schemas and task descriptions rather than raw patient records. A tool-augmented generation mechanism equips the LLM with specialized routines for querying irregular temporal data, enabling it to produce executable feature-extraction code that explicitly handles uneven observation patterns and informative sparsity. FeatEHR-LLM supports both univariate and multivariate feature generation through an iterative, validation-in-the-loop pipeline. Evaluated on eight clinical prediction tasks across four ICU datasets, our framework achieves the highest mean AUROC on 7 out of 8 tasks, with improvements of up to 6 percentage points over strong baselines. Code is available at github.com/hojjatkarami/FeatEHR-LLM.

</details>


### [38] [An Integrated Framework for Explainable, Fair, and Observable Hospital Readmission Prediction: Development and Validation on MIMIC-IV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22535)
*Isaac Tosin Adisa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并回顾性验证了一个集成框架，解决再入院预测临床转化的三大障碍：缺乏可解释性、缺乏部署可靠性基础设施以及人口统计学公平性评估不足。在MIMIC-IV数据库415231例成人入院数据上，XGBoost模型AUC-ROC达0.696，LightGBM校准最佳（Brier 0.146），所有亚组均满足公平性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有再入院预测模型在临床转化中存在三个关键障碍：模型缺乏可解释性（黑箱问题）、缺乏可靠的部署基础设施、未充分评估不同人口统计学亚群间的公平性。本文旨在构建一个同时解决这三方面问题的集成框架。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV数据库构建415231例成人入院队列（30天再入院率18.0%），按70/15/15划分训练集、验证集和测试集。训练逻辑回归、XGBoost和LightGBM三个模型，使用26个特征。采用SHAP进行逐例解释，在16个亚组中评估公平性（AUC-ROC、假阴性率FNR、阳性预测值PPV），使用Brier分数和校准曲线评估校准度。

Result: XGBoost达到AUC-ROC 0.696（95% CI 0.691-0.701），优于或匹敌LACE基线（AUC 0.60-0.68）。LightGBM校准最佳（Brier 0.146）。既往入院次数是最重要的预测因子。所有亚组均满足公平性阈值（delta AUC ≤ 0.05, delta FNR ≤ 0.10）。

Conclusion: 该框架在预测性能、临床可操作的解释性和人口统计学公平性方面均表现优异，代码已公开。

Abstract: Objective: To propose and retrospectively validate an integrated framework addressing three barriers to clinical translation of readmission prediction: lack of explainability, absence of deployment reliability infrastructure, and inadequate demographic fairness evaluation.
  Materials and Methods: We constructed a cohort of 415231 adult admissions from the MIMIC-IV database (30-day readmission prevalence 18.0%), split 70/15/15. Logistic regression, XGBoost, and LightGBM models were trained on 26 features. SHAP provided per-patient explanations. Fairness was evaluated across 16 subgroups using AUC-ROC, false negative rate (FNR), and positive predictive value (PPV). Calibration was assessed using Brier scores and calibration curves.
  Results: XGBoost achieved AUC-ROC 0.696 (95% CI 0.691-0.701), outperforming or matching the LACE baseline (AUC 0.60-0.68). LightGBM achieved best calibration (Brier 0.146). Prior admissions were the dominant predictor. All subgroups met equity thresholds (delta AUC <= 0.05, delta FNR <= 0.10).
  Conclusion: This framework delivers competitive performance, clinically actionable explanations, and strong demographic equity. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tomisin92/readmission-prediction.

</details>


### [39] [SOLAR-RL: Semi-Online Long-horizon Assignment Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22558)
*Jichao Wang,Liuyang Bian,Yufeng Zhou,Han Xiao,Yue Pan,Guozhi Wang,Hao Wang,Zhaoxiong Wang,Yafei Wen,Xiaoxin Chen,Shuai Ren,Lingfang Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLAR-RL提出了一种半在线长程任务分配强化学习框架，通过从静态数据中重构轨迹、检测首个失效点并回溯分配密集步骤级奖励，在无需在线交互成本的情况下模拟在线反馈，显著提升了GUI智能体在长程任务中的完成率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离线RL忽略了全局轨迹语义（如任务完成度和执行质量），而在线RL虽能捕获长程动态但交互成本高且环境不稳定。现有方法难以在样本效率和任务性能之间取得平衡。

Method: 从静态数据中重构多样化rollout候选轨迹；利用每步有效性信号检测首个失效点；通过目标对齐的奖励塑形回溯分配密集步骤级奖励，以反映轨迹级执行质量，从而在不增加交互成本的情况下模拟在线反馈。

Result: 在长程GUI导航任务上，SOLAR-RL相比强基线方法显著提升了任务完成率和鲁棒性，验证了其样本高效性。

Conclusion: SOLAR-RL为自主GUI导航提供了一种样本高效的解决方案，弥合了离线RL忽视轨迹语义和在线RL高交互成本之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) mature, GUI agents are evolving from static interactions to complex navigation. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training MLLM agents on dynamic GUI tasks, its effective application faces a dilemma. Standard Offline RL often relies on static step-level data, neglecting global trajectory semantics such as task completion and execution quality. Conversely, Online RL captures the long-term dynamics but suffers from high interaction costs and potential environmental instability. To bridge this gap, we propose SOLAR-RL (Semi-Online Long-horizon Assignment Reinforcement Learning). Instead of relying solely on expensive online interactions, our framework integrates global trajectory insights directly into the offline learning process. Specifically, we reconstruct diverse rollout candidates from static data, detect the first failure point using per-step validity signals, and retroactively assign dense step-level rewards with target-aligned shaping to reflect trajectory-level execution quality, effectively simulating online feedback without interaction costs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SOLAR-RL significantly improves long-horizon task completion rates and robustness compared to strong baselines, offering a sample-efficient solution for autonomous GUI navigation.

</details>


### [40] [Data-Free Contribution Estimation in Federated Learning using Gradient von Neumann Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22562)
*Asim Ukaye,Mubarak Abdu-Aguye,Nurbek Tastan,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于矩阵冯·诺依曼（谱）熵的无数据客户端贡献估计方法，通过最终层更新的信息多样性度量客户端重要性，并实例化了两种方案：SpectralFed（归一化熵作为聚合权重）和SpectralFuse（熵与类别对齐融合结合秩自适应卡尔曼滤波）。实验表明该方法与客户端准确率高度相关，优于现有无数据基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中现有客户端贡献估计方法依赖服务端验证数据或客户端自报告信息，这要么损害隐私，要么易受操纵，因此需要一种无需额外数据且抗操纵的贡献估计信号。

Method: 基于最终层更新的矩阵谱熵衡量信息多样性，提出两种方案：SpectralFed直接将归一化熵作为聚合权重；SpectralFuse通过秩自适应卡尔曼滤波将熵与类别对齐融合，实现每轮稳定性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、FEMNIST和FedISIC基准上，熵分数与客户端独立准确率高度相关，无需验证数据或客户端元数据，且优于无数据贡献估计基线。

Conclusion: 谱熵是一种有效的客户端贡献指示器，能在保护隐私的前提下实现公平奖励和稳健聚合。

Abstract: Client contribution estimation in Federated Learning is necessary for identifying clients' importance and for providing fair rewards. Current methods often rely on server-side validation data or self-reported client information, which can compromise privacy or be susceptible to manipulation. We introduce a data-free signal based on the matrix von Neumann (spectral) entropy of the final-layer updates, which measures the diversity of the information contributed. We instantiate two practical schemes: (i) SpectralFed, which uses normalized entropy as aggregation weights, and (ii) SpectralFuse, which fuses entropy with class-specific alignment via a rank-adaptive Kalman filter for per-round stability. Across CIFAR-10/100 and the naturally partitioned FEMNIST and FedISIC benchmarks, entropy-derived scores show a consistently high correlation with standalone client accuracy under diverse non-IID regimes - without validation data or client metadata. We compare our results with data-free contribution estimation baselines and show that spectral entropy serves as a useful indicator of client contribution.

</details>


### [41] [SpikingBrain2.0: Brain-Inspired Foundation Models for Efficient Long-Context and Cross-Platform Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22575)
*Yuqi Pan,Jinghao Zhuang,Yupeng Feng,Fangzhi Zhong,Siyu Ding,Xuerui Qiu,Shaowei Gu,Bohan Sun,Zhiyong Qin,Yibo Zhong,Lingtao Ouyang,Kun Yang,Zehao Liu,Yuhong Chou,Shurong Wang,Anjie Hu,Han Xu,Bo Xu,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpikingBrain2.0 (SpB2.0) 是一个5B参数的多模态脉冲基础模型，通过提出双空间稀疏注意力（DSSA）架构和优化的Transformer-to-Hybrid（T2H）训练策略，在长序列场景下实现了显著的效率提升。该模型支持INT8脉冲编码和FP8编码双量化路径，在4M上下文下实现10.13x TTFT加速，可支持超过10M token处理，并在GPU和神经形态硬件上均表现出良好的跨平台兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展上下文长度对大型模型发展至关重要，但全注意力Transformer在长序列场景下存在计算和推理瓶颈。现有方法难以在保持性能的同时实现长上下文效率与最小化训练开销，亟需设计兼具脑启发机制与高效架构的基础模型。

Method: (1) 架构创新：提出DSSA（双空间稀疏注意力），即层间混合的稀疏Softmax注意力（MoBA）与稀疏线性注意力（SSE），实现更好的性能-效率权衡；支持INT8脉冲编码（稀疏事件驱动计算）和FP8编码（GPU加速）双量化路径。(2) 训练策略：开发优化后的Transformer-to-Hybrid（T2H）流水线，包含LLM和VLM的双转换路径，使用精选开源数据。

Result: SpB2.0-5B和SpB2.0-VL-5B在不到7k A100 GPU训练时长下恢复基础Transformer（Qwen3-4B）大部分能力；4M上下文下TTFT加速10.13x；8块A100 GPU上支持超过10M token（vLLM），而全注意力模型超出内存限制；FP8 GPU推理在250k上下文下加速2.52x；神经形态执行达到64.31%稀疏度，500MHz下面积和功耗分别降低70.6%和46.5%。

Conclusion: SpikingBrain2.0为轻量级、多模态脉冲基础模型提供了实用路径，展示了将脑启发机制与高效架构相结合在资源受限和边缘场景下的潜力。

Abstract: Scaling context length is reshaping large-model development, yet full-attention Transformers suffer from prohibitive computation and inference bottlenecks at long sequences. A key challenge is to design foundation models that maintain performance and long-context efficiency with minimal training overhead. We introduce SpikingBrain2.0 (SpB2.0), a 5B model that advances both architecture and training efficiency of its predecessor.
  Our contributions are two-fold. (1) Architectural Innovation: We propose Dual-Space Sparse Attention (DSSA), an inter-layer hybrid of Sparse Softmax Attention (MoBA) and Sparse Linear Attention (SSE), achieving an improved performance-efficiency trade-off for long-context modeling. SpB2.0 further supports dual quantization paths: INT8-Spiking coding enables sparse event-driven computation, while FP8 coding accelerates inference on modern GPUs. (2) Enhanced Training Strategy: We develop an optimized Transformer-to-Hybrid (T2H) pipeline with dual conversion paths for LLMs and VLMs using curated open-source data.
  Empirically, SpB2.0-5B and SpB2.0-VL-5B recover most of the base Transformer (Qwen3-4B) capability with under 7k A100 GPU hours. SpB2.0 achieves a 10.13x TTFT speedup at 4M context and supports over 10M tokens on 8 A100 GPUs under vLLM, where full-attention models exceed memory limits. It also demonstrates strong cross-platform compatibility, enabling FP8 GPU inference (2.52x speedup at 250k) and efficient neuromorphic execution (64.31% sparsity, with 70.6% and 46.5% area and power reduction at 500MHz).
  Overall, SpikingBrain2.0 provides a practical pathway for lightweight, multimodal, spiking foundation models, highlighting the potential of combining brain-inspired mechanisms with efficient architectures for resource-constrained and edge scenarios.

</details>


### [42] [Adaptive Head Budgeting for Efficient Multi-Head Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22583)
*Bilal Faye,Abdoulaye Mbaye,Hanane Azzag,Mustapha Lebbah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BudgetFormer提出了一种自适应多头注意力机制，通过为每个输入动态分配注意力头数量和相关重要性分布，在文本分类任务中同时降低了计算开销（FLOPs和内存）并提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多头注意力对所有输入使用固定数量的注意力头，忽视了输入复杂度和任务需求的差异，导致粗粒度任务（如文本分类）中计算资源浪费或性能次优。

Method: 提出BudgetFormer架构，包含：(1) 为每个输入学习一个头预算（所需注意力头数量）和一个相关性分布（选择最具信息量的头）；(2) 基于探索-利用权衡的训练策略，先探索有效的头配置再收敛到高效模式。

Result: 在多种复杂度的文本分类任务上，BudgetFormer降低了推理阶段的FLOPs和内存消耗，同时在性能上可超越标准全多头注意力。

Conclusion: 自适应头分配是一种提升Transformer模型效率和效果的原则性方法，展示了通过动态资源分配在保持甚至提升性能的同时降低计算成本的潜力。

Abstract: Transformers have become the dominant architecture across a wide range of domains, largely due to the effectiveness of multi-head attention in capturing diverse representation subspaces. However, standard multi-head attention activates all heads uniformly for every input, regardless of task requirements or input complexity. In many scenarios, particularly for coarse-grained tasks such as text classification, the relevant information is often global and does not require the full diversity of attention heads. As a consequence, using a fixed number of heads can introduce unnecessary computational cost or lead to suboptimal performance when the allocation does not match the input. To address this limitation, we introduce BudgetFormer, a Transformer architecture equipped with an adaptive multi-head attention mechanism that dynamically allocates computational resources. Our approach learns, for each input, both a head budget corresponding to the number of attention heads required, and a relevance distribution that selects the most informative heads. We also propose a training strategy based on an exploration and exploitation trade-off, allowing the model to discover effective head configurations before converging to efficient usage patterns. Experiments on text classification tasks of varying complexity show that our method reduces inference cost in terms of FLOPs and memory, while also achieving performance that can surpass standard full multi-head attention. These results highlight the potential of adaptive head allocation as a principled approach to improving both efficiency and effectiveness in Transformer models.

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Patient Invariance: Learning Cardiac Dynamics via Action-Conditioned JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22618)
*Jose Geraldo Fernandes,Luiz Facury,Pedro Robles Dutenhefner,Wagner Meira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有医疗自监督学习（基于不变性目标）与临床诊断本质冲突，提出基于事件条件的世界模型（Event-Conditioned World Models），将病理定义为作用于患者潜在状态的转移向量，通过预测疾病发作后的心脏电生理状态，在MIMIC-IV-ECG数据集上超越全监督基线，低资源场景下AUROC提升超0.05。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗自监督学习基于不变性目标（invariance-based objectives），最大化同一患者不同视图的相似性，这在数学上迫使模型抑制其本应检测的瞬态病理变化，与临床诊断的核心理念相悖。

Method: 提出Event-Conditioned World Models，将病理定义为作用于患者潜在状态的转移向量（transition vector），基于LeJEPA框架适配生理时间序列，预测疾病发作后心脏的未来电生理状态，从而解耦稳定解剖特征与动态病理力量。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-ECG数据集上，该方法在关键分诊任务中超越全监督基线，且在低资源场景下AUROC提升超过0.05，展现出优越的样本效率。

Conclusion: 建模生物动态过程能提供比静态分类更鲁棒的密集监督信号，方向正确且有效。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning in healthcare has largely relied on invariance-based objectives, which maximize similarity between different views of the same patient. While effective for static anatomy, this paradigm is fundamentally misaligned with clinical diagnosis, as it mathematically compels the model to suppress the transient pathological changes it is intended to detect. We propose a shift towards Action-Conditioned World Models that learn to simulate the dynamics of disease progression, or Event-Conditioned. Adapting the LeJEPA framework to physiological time-series, we define pathology not as a static label, but as a transition vector acting on a patient's latent state. By predicting the future electrophysiological state of the heart given a disease onset, our model explicitly disentangles stable anatomical features from dynamic pathological forces. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV-ECG dataset, our approach outperforms fully supervised baselines on the critical triage task. Crucially, we demonstrate superior sample efficiency: in low-resource regimes, our world model outperforms supervised learning by over 0.05 AUROC. These results suggest that modeling biological dynamics provides a dense supervision signal that is far more robust than static classification. Source code is available at https://github.com/cljosegfer/lesaude-dynamics

</details>


### [44] [Associativity-Peakiness Metric for Contingency Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22655)
*Naomi E. Zirkind,William J. Diehl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估聚类算法输出（列联表）的新指标——关联峰值度（AP），该指标能更高效、更高动态范围地表征聚类算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类算法性能评估缺乏针对列联表的单一度量指标，现有向量对度量无法揭示列联表中的细粒度特征，亟需一种能全面刻画聚类算法性能的度量方法。

Method: 提出Associativity Peakiness (AP)指标，类比监督学习中的混淆矩阵质量度量，用于表征列联表中聚类算法性能的关键方面。通过模拟生成500个列联表进行多场景测试验证。

Result: AP指标在评估聚类算法时比现有公开指标具有更高的动态范围，且计算效率更高。

Conclusion: AP指标可作为聚类算法性能比较的有效度量工具，尤其在列联表输出场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: For the use case of comparing the performance of clustering algorithms whose output is a contingency table, a single performance metric for contingency tables is needed. Such a metric is vital for comparative performance analysis of clustering algorithms. A survey of publicly available literature did not show the presence of such a metric. Metrics do exist for vector pairs of truth values and predicted values, which are an alternative form of output of clustering algorithms. However, the metrics for vector pairs do not reveal the presence of detailed features that are apparent in contingency tables. This paper presents the Associativity Peakiness (AP) metric, which characterizes aspects of clustering algorithm performance that are critical for predicting a clustering algorithm's performance when deployed. The AP metric is analogous to measures of quality for confusion matrices that are outputs of supervised learning algorithms. This paper presents results from simulations in which 500 contingency tables were generated for multiple test scenarios. The results show that for the use case of evaluating clustering algorithms, the AP metric characterizes performance of contingency tables with higher dynamic range than publicly available metrics, and that it is computationally more efficient than comparable publicly available metrics.

</details>


### [45] [Iterative Model-Learning Scheme via Gaussian Processes for Nonlinear Model Predictive Control of (Semi-)Batch Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22672)
*Tai Xuan Tan,Alexander Mitsos,Eike Cramer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯过程（GP）的模型学习非线性模型预测控制（GP-MLMPC）方案，用于间歇过程控制。该方法仅需一条初始轨迹数据（如PI控制器数据）即可初始化，通过迭代运行NMPC并更新GP模型，实现批次间性能提升。利用GP的不确定性量化构建机会约束，确保安全运行。在半间歇聚合反应器上的仿真表明：跟踪目标下，仅4次迭代后跟踪误差较初始轨迹降低83%；经济目标下，第8次迭代最终产物质量提升17倍。性能与基于完整机理模型的NMPC相当，且样本效率高、收敛快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇过程具有本质瞬态和非线性特性，适合采用非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），但NMPC的实际应用受限于动态模型的高成本和不可获得性。为此，本文旨在提出一种无需机理模型、仅依赖数据驱动的控制方案。

Method: 提出GP-MLMPC方案：1）用单条初始轨迹（如PI控制器数据）初始化GP模型；2）在NMPC中嵌入GP模型进行控制，每次迭代后利用新观测数据更新GP；3）利用GP的不确定性量化构建机会约束，满足指定置信水平的安全约束。

Result: 在半间歇聚合反应器仿真中：跟踪目标下，4次迭代后跟踪误差降低83%；经济目标下，8次迭代后最终产物质量提升17倍。性能与基于完整机理模型的NMPC相当。

Conclusion: GP-MLMPC方案是一种有前景的数据高效方法，可在无需机理知识的情况下实现对非线性间歇过程的最优控制，且样本效率高、收敛快。

Abstract: Batch processes are inherently transient and typically nonlinear, motivating nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC). However, adopting NMPC is hindered by the cost and unavailability of dynamic models. Thus, we propose to use Gaussian Processes (GP) in a model-learning NMPC scheme (GP-MLMPC) for batch processes. We initialize the GP-MLMPC using data from a single initial trajectory, e.g., from a PI controller. We iteratively apply the NMPC embedded with GPs to run batches and update the GP with new observations from each iteration, thereby achieving batch-wise improvements. Using uncertainty quantification from the GPs, we formulate chance constraints to enforce safe operation to the required confidence levels. We demonstrate our approach in \textit{silico} on a semi-batch polymerization reactor for tracking and economic objectives over durations of two hours, and the reactor temperature is constrained in a range of $\pm2^\circ C$ around its setpoint. After only four batch iterations, tracking error from the GP-MLMPC scheme converged to a reduction of $83\%$, compared to the initial trajectory. Furthermore, under an economic objective, the GP-MLMPC resulted in a 17-fold increase in final product mass by iteration 8, compared to the initial trajectory. In both cases, the resulting GP-MLMPC performance is on par with the full-model NMPC, which shows that the optimal controller can be learned by the approach. By collecting samples around the optimal trajectory, the GP-MLMPC remains sample-efficient across iterations and achieves quick convergence. Thus, the proposed GP-MLMPC scheme presents a promising data-efficient approach for the control of nonlinear batch processes without mechanistic knowledge.

</details>


### [46] [Operational Feature Fingerprints of Graph Datasets via a White-Box Signal-Subspace Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22676)
*Yuchen Xiong,Swee Keong Yeap,Zhen Hong Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WG-SRC，一种白盒信号子空间探针，用固定图信号字典替代学习型消息传递，实现可解释的节点分类和数据集诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN的节点分类过程将多种信号（自属性、邻域平滑、高通差异、类几何、分类器边界）纠缠在不可解释的表示中，无法解释分类原因及数据集所需的学习机制。

Method: WG-SRC使用固定图信号字典（原始特征、行归一化/对称归一化低通传播、高通差异），结合Fisher坐标选择、类-wise PCA子空间、闭形式多α岭分类和验证分数融合，实现白盒预测与分析。

Result: 在6个节点分类数据集上与图基线方法竞争力相当，平均增益为正。生成的行为图谱将预测分解为原始特征、低通、高通、类几何和岭边界成分。

Conclusion: WG-SRC可作为图数据集诊断工具，识别低通主导（Amazon）、混合高通+类几何复杂（Chameleon）、原始/边界敏感（WebKB）等数据集特征指纹，并为后续分析和模型修改提供指导。

Abstract: Graph neural networks achieve strong node-classification accuracy, but their learned message passing entangles ego attributes, neighborhood smoothing, high-pass graph differences, class geometry, and classifier boundaries in an opaque representation. This obscures why a node is classified and what feature-level graph-learning mechanisms a dataset requires.
  We propose WG-SRC, a white-box signal-subspace probe for prediction and graph dataset diagnosis. WG-SRC replaces learned message passing with a fixed, named graph-signal dictionary of raw features, row-normalized and symmetric-normalized low-pass propagation, and high-pass graph differences. It combines Fisher coordinate selection, class-wise PCA subspaces, closed-form multi-alpha ridge classification, and validation-based score fusion, so prediction and analysis use explicit class subspaces, energy-controlled dimensions, and closed-form linear decisions.
  As a white-box graph-learning instrument, WG-SRC uses predictive performance to validate its diagnostics: across six node-classification datasets, the scaffold remains competitive with reproduced graph baselines and achieves positive average gain under aligned splits. Its atlas, produced by a predictor, decomposes behavior into raw-feature, low-pass, high-pass, class-geometric, and ridge-boundary components. These operational feature fingerprints distinguish low-pass-dominated Amazon graphs, mixed high-pass and class-geometrically complex Chameleon behavior, and raw- or boundary-sensitive WebKB graphs. As intrinsic classifier outputs rather than post-hoc explanations, these fingerprints provide post-evaluation guidance for later analysis and dataset-specific modification. Aligned mechanistic interventions support this guidance by indicating when high-pass blocks act as removable noise, when raw features should be preserved, and when ridge-type boundary correction matters.

</details>


### [47] [Zero-Shot Morphological Discovery in Low-Resource Bantu Languages via Cross-Lingual Transfer and Unsupervised Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22723)
*Hillary Mutisya,John Mugane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结合跨语言迁移学习与无监督聚类的方法，在低资源班图语（Giriama）中自动发现名词分类、形态前缀模式及词干切分，仅用91个标注范例即实现高准确率的形态学分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源班图语（如Giriama）缺乏标注数据，传统形态学分析方法难以适用，亟需能在极少标注样本下自动发现形态特征的方法，以支持语言文档化与濒危语言保护。

Method: 集成斯瓦希里语的跨语言迁移学习与无监督聚类，通过加权投票融合两者结果：迁移学习利用约60%的同源词进行类别检测，无监督聚类发现语言特有的创新形态特征。流程包括名词类别发现、前缀模式识别（元音合并a-变体、缩略k'-前缀）、语料库扩展及词干切分与词形还原。

Result: 在Giriama上：发现2,455个词的名词类别分配；识别出两种未记录形态模式（a-前缀变体95.1%一致性、k'-前缀98.5%一致性）；444个动词范例词形还原准确率78.2%；v3语料扩展至19,624词（9,014唯一词干），切分率97.3%，词形还原率86.7%。

Conclusion: 跨语言迁移与无监督聚类的组合策略能有效从极少量标注数据中挖掘低资源语言的形态特征，兼具同源词检测与语言特有创新发现的双重优势，为班图语形态学文档化提供可复现的工具。

Abstract: We present a method for discovering morphological features in low-resource Bantu languages by combining cross-lingual transfer learning with unsupervised clustering. Applied to Giriama (nyf), a language with only 91 labeled paradigms, our pipeline discovers noun class assignments for 2,455 words and identifies two previously undocumented morphological patterns: an a- prefix variant for Class 2 (vowel coalescence - the merger of two adjacent vowels - of wa-, 95.1% consistency) and a contracted k'- prefix (98.5% consistency). External validation on 444 known Giriama verb paradigms confirms 78.2% lemmatization accuracy, while a v3 corpus expansion to 19,624 words (9,014 unique lemmas) achieves 97.3% segmentation and 86.7% lemmatization rates across all major word classes. Our ensemble of transfer learning from Swahili and unsupervised clustering, combined via weighted voting, exploits complementary strengths: transfer excels at cognate detection (leveraging ~60% vocabulary overlap) while clustering discovers language-specific innovations invisible to transfer. We release all code and discovered lexicons to support morphological documentation for low-resource Bantu languages.

</details>


### [48] [Spend Less, Fit Better: Budget-Efficient Scaling Law Fitting via Active Experiment Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22753)
*Sijie Li,Shanda Li,Haowei Lin,Weiwei Sun,Ameet Talwalkar,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将缩放律拟合视为预算感知的序列实验设计问题，通过不确定性感知方法在有限预算下选择最有价值的实验，仅用约10%的训练预算即可接近全量实验的拟合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大规模训练中，收集足够信息量的试点实验本身已成为重大预算分配问题，传统方法将拟合缩放律视为常规预处理步骤，忽视了实验成本差异和预算约束。

Method: 将缩放律拟合形式化为预算感知的序列实验设计问题，提出不确定性感知方法，在有限实验池中根据成本和信息价值顺序分配预算，优先选择对高成本目标区域外推最有用的实验。

Result: 在多种缩放律任务基准上，该方法一致优于经典设计基线，仅使用约10%总训练预算即可接近全量实验集的拟合性能。

Conclusion: 预算感知的序列实验设计能大幅降低缩放律拟合成本，为百万级训练规划提供了更经济高效的实验策略。

Abstract: Scaling laws are used to plan multi-million-dollar training runs, but fitting those laws can itself cost millions. In modern large-scale workflows, assembling a sufficiently informative set of pilot experiments is already a major budget-allocation problem rather than a routine preprocessing step. We formulate scaling-law fitting as budget-aware sequential experimental design: given a finite pool of runnable experiments with heterogeneous costs, choose which runs to execute so as to maximize extrapolation accuracy in a high-cost target region. We then propose an uncertainty-aware method for sequentially allocating experimental budget toward the runs most useful for target-region extrapolation. Across a diverse benchmark of scaling-law tasks, our method consistently outperforms classical design-based baselines, and often approaches the performance of fitting on the full experimental set while using only about 10% of the total training budget. Our code is available at https://github.com/PlanarG/active-sl.

</details>

### [49] [Can SOC Operators Explain their Decisions while Triaging Alarms? A Real-World Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22001)
*Jessica Moosmann,Irdin Pekaric,Giovanni Apruzzese*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统文献综述（257篇文献）和实地调研（12名SOC员工）发现，虽然安全运营中心分析师能正确区分真实与误报警告（83%准确率），但仅有39%的案例能提供正确的解释理由，揭示了当前决策支持系统在帮助分析师理解并阐述判断依据方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大量关注SOC的内部流程、挑战以及分析师面临的告警疲劳问题，但很少有人探究一个关键问题：SOC员工在为告警进行分类（triage）时，是否能够证明（justify）其决策的正确性？回答这一问题对未来改进工具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 对257篇研究文献进行系统性文献综述；2) 与真实SOC合作开展实地研究（n=12），向SOC员工展示真实告警，要求判断是否属于真实安全事件，并解释其决策依据。

Result: 分析师在83%的案例中正确区分了真实与误报告警，但仅有39%的解释反映了实际根本原因，提供了正确的理由。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当前SOC需要不仅能帮助分析师做出正确判断，还能帮助他们理解并阐明判断为何正确的决策支持系统。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) are pivotal in modern enterprises. Tasked to monitor complex network environments constantly under attack, SOCs can be active 24/7 and can include hundreds of operators supported by state-of-the-art technologies. Abundant research has studied the internal processes of SOCs, highlighting their pros and cons, as well as the challenges faced by SOC analysts -- such as dealing with the overwhelming number of false alarms triggered by automated security mechanisms. In this context, we wonder: given that "someone" must triage the alarms, and that such triaging must be grounded on established knowledge or evidence-based reasoning, can SOC employees justify why a certain decision was taken while triaging alarms? Answering such a research question (RQ) can better guide future efforts.
  We hence tackle this RQs. First, via a systematic literature review across 257 research documents, we provide evidence that such RQ received limited attention so far. Then, we partner-up with a real-world SOC and carry out a field study (n=12) with SOC employees. We show them real alarms raised in their SOC, and inquire whether such alarms are indicative of true security problems or not. Then, we ask to explain their decision. We found that while most analysts were able to separate "true from false" alarms (the decision was correct in 83% of the cases), a correct justification was hardly provided (only 39% of the provided explanations reflected the actual root cause). Ultimately, our results highlight the need for decision-support systems that help SOC analysts not only make the right call -- but also understand and articulate why it is right.

</details>


### [50] [Sovereign Agentic Loops: Decoupling AI Reasoning from Execution in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22136)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出主权智能体循环（SAL），一种控制平面架构，将模型输出与执行层解耦，通过结构化意图、验证、混淆膜和证据链实现策略约束执行、身份隔离和确定性重放，在云基础设施原型中阻止93%的不安全意图，增加12.4ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体直接使用随机模型输出执行API调用，由于模型正确性、上下文感知和对齐性无法保证，存在安全风险。

Method: SAL架构：模型输出结构化意图+理由，控制平面验证意图（基于真实系统状态和策略），混淆膜限制模型访问身份敏感状态，密码学链接的证据链实现可审计和重放。

Result: OpenKedge原型中，策略层阻止93%不安全意图，一致性检查拒绝剩余7%，基准测试中防止不安全执行，中位延迟增加12.4ms。

Conclusion: SAL在假设条件下提供策略约束执行、身份隔离和确定性重放，有效提升LLM智能体的安全性，代价是可接受的延迟开销。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly issue API calls that mutate real systems, yet many current architectures pass stochastic model outputs directly to execution layers. We argue that this coupling creates a safety risk because model correctness, context awareness, and alignment cannot be assumed at execution time. We introduce Sovereign Agentic Loops (SAL), a control-plane architecture in which models emit structured intents with justifications, and the control plane validates those intents against true system state and policy before execution. SAL combines an obfuscation membrane, which limits model access to identity-sensitive state, with a cryptographically linked Evidence Chain for auditability and replay. We formalize SAL and show that, under the stated assumptions, it provides policy-bounded execution, identity isolation, and deterministic replay. In an OpenKedge prototype for cloud infrastructure, SAL blocks 93% of unsafe intents at the policy layer, rejects the remaining 7% via consistency checks, prevents unsafe executions in our benchmark, and adds 12.4 ms median latency.

</details>


### [51] [PrivSTRUCT: Untangling Data Purpose Compliance of Privacy Policies in Google Play Store](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22157)
*Bhanuka Silva,Anirban Mahanti,Aruna Seneviratne,Suranga Senevirante*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivSTRUCT框架，利用文档层次结构（标题引导）来解缠隐私政策中的复杂披露。相比PoliGrapher，提取的数据项和目的片段数量翻倍。对3,756个Android应用的大规模分析揭示了透明度缺口：全局定义目的时，第一方收集夸大目的概率高20.4%，第三方共享高9.7%；敏感第三方数据流（如金融数据用于分析）常被稀释和混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将隐私政策视为扁平化文本，忽略文档的逻辑层次结构（如章节标题），导致自动化方法将不同数据实践（特别是敏感数据项与其特定目的）纠缠混淆。

Method: 提出PrivSTRUCT，一种新型系统性编码器-解码器组合框架，利用开发者定义的层次结构线索（章节标题）来解缠复杂隐私披露。

Result: 1）相比PoliGrapher，PrivSTRUCT提取的数据项和目的片段数量翻倍；2）对3,756个Android应用分析发现，全局定义目的时第一方收集夸大目的概率高20.4%，第三方共享高9.7%；3）敏感第三方数据流（如金融数据用于分析）常被稀释归类为通用或无关类别。

Conclusion: 当前隐私目的披露存在系统性失败，尤其是全局目的声明导致透明度缺口，且敏感第三方数据共享的目的披露被模糊化处理。

Abstract: Existing research typically treats privacy policies as flat, uniform text, extracting information without regard for the document's logical hierarchy. Disregard for structural cues of section headings designed to guide the reader, often leads automated methods to entangle distinct data practices, particularly when linking sensitive data items to their specific purposes. To address this, we introduce PrivSTRUCT, a novel and systematic encoder and decoder combined framework that to untangle complex privacy disclosures. Benchmarking against the state-of-the-art tool PoliGrapher reveals that PrivSTRUCT robustly extracts more than x2 the number of data item and purpose excerpts while retaining developer-defined structural cues. By applying PrivSTRUCT to a large-scale dataset of 3,756 Android apps, we uncover a critical transparency gap: the probability of developers overstating a data purpose is 20.4% higher for first-party collection and 9.7% higher for third-party sharing when they rely on globally defined purposes rather than specific, locally scoped disclosures. Alarmingly, we find that sensitive third-party data flows such as sharing financial data for analytics are frequently diluted and entangled into generic or unrelated categories, highlighting a persistent failure in the current purpose disclosure landscape.

</details>


### [52] [FixV2W: Correcting Invalid CVE-CWE Mappings with Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22176)
*Sevval Simsek,Varsha Athreya,David Starobinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FixV2W是一种轻量级方法，利用知识图谱嵌入和纵向趋势分析，通过历史重映射模式和层次关系来修正NVD中不准确或不完整的CVE到CWE映射，实验表明在69%的被利用漏洞中预测出正确CWE，并将ML模型的MRR从0.174提升至0.608。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共数据库（如NVD）中的CVE到CWE映射存在不一致和不完整的问题，这阻碍了自动化分析和漏洞修复，因此需要一种方法来提高映射准确性。

Method: FixV2W采用知识图谱嵌入和纵向趋势分析，系统性地研究历史重映射模式，利用NVD和CWE数据中的层次关系，为链接到禁止或废弃类别的漏洞预测更精确的CWE映射。

Result: 在2021年8月至2024年12月的数据集上，Top 10预测中FixV2W对69%的原无效CWE被利用漏洞预测正确；将未知CVE-CWE映射模型的MRR从0.174提升至0.608。

Conclusion: FixV2W是一种有前景的方法，能够有效识别和应对新兴威胁，显著提升依赖NVD数据的ML模型性能。

Abstract: Accurate mapping between Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) and Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) entries is critical for effective vulnerability management and risk assessment. However, public databases, such as the National Vulnerability Database (NVD), suffer from inconsistent and incomplete CVE to CWE mappings, complicating automated analysis and remediation. We introduce FixV2W, a lightweight approach that leverages knowledge graph embeddings and longitudinal trends to improve mapping accuracy of the NVD. FixV2W systematically analyzes historical remapping patterns and leverages hierarchical relationships within NVD and CWE data to predict more precise CWE mappings for vulnerabilities linked to Prohibited or Discouraged categories. We run extensive experimental evaluation of FixV2W, based on test data set collected between August 2021 and December 2024. Considering the Top 10 ranked predictions, the results show that FixV2W predicts the correct CWE mappings for 69% of exploited vulnerabilities that had invalid CWEs before they were exploited. We also show that FixV2W significantly improves the performance of ML models relying on NVD data. For instance, for a model geared at uncovering unknown CVE-CWE mappings, FixV2W improves the Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) from 0.174 to 0.608. These results show that FixV2W is a promising approach to identify and thwart emerging threats.

</details>


### [53] [Behavioral Canaries: Auditing Private Retrieved Context Usage in RL Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22191)
*Chaoran Chen,Dayu Yuan,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出行为金丝雀(Behavioral Canaries)机制，通过在偏好数据中注入触发条件与特定风格的反馈配对，使审计者能够检测RL微调管道中是否存在未经授权的受保护文档训练行为，在1%注入率下达到67%检测率（10%假阳性率，AUROC=0.756）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计方法（如逐字记忆检测、成员推理）依赖事实性记忆痕迹，但RL训练主要影响模型的behaviral style而非具体事实记忆，导致传统方法对RL微调模型无效。审计者缺乏可靠手段验证提供商是否违反服务条款，将受保护检索上下文集用于RL后训练。

Method: 设计行为金丝雀机制：在偏好数据中将文档触发器与奖励特定风格响应的反馈配对，诱导出潜伏的触发条件偏好。若该数据被用于训练，模型会产生可检测的分布性行为偏移，而非事实记忆。

Result: 在1%金丝雀注入率下，检测率达67%（假阳性率10%），AUROC=0.756，验证了行为信号能够有效检测未经授权的文档条件训练。

Conclusion: 行为金丝雀为RLFT管道建立了一种新的审计机制，使审计者能够检测训练时影响，即使这种影响表现为分布性行为变化而非记忆化。

Abstract: In agentic workflows, LLMs frequently process retrieved contexts that are legally protected from further training. However, auditors currently lack a reliable way to verify if a provider has violated the terms of service by incorporating these data into post-training, especially through Reinforcement Learning (RL). While standard auditing relies on verbatim memorization and membership inference, these methods are ineffective for RL-trained models, as RL primarily influences a model's behavioral style rather than the retention of specific facts. To bridge this gap, we introduce Behavioral Canaries, a new auditing mechanism for RLFT pipelines. The framework instruments preference data by pairing document triggers with feedback that rewards a distinctive stylistic response, inducing a latent trigger-conditioned preference if such data are used in training. Empirical results show that these behavioral signals enable detection of unauthorized document-conditioned training, achieving a 67% detection rate at a 10% false-positive rate (AUROC = 0.756) at a 1% canary injection rate. More broadly, our results establish behavioral canaries as a new auditing mechanism for RLFT pipelines, enabling auditors to test for training-time influence even when such influence manifests as distributional behavioral change rather than memorization.

</details>


### [54] [Train in Vain: Functionality-Preserving Poisoning to Prevent Unauthorized Use of Code Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22291)
*Yuan Xiao,Jiaming Wang,Yuchen Chen,Wei Song,Jun Sun,Shiqing Ma,Yanzhou Mu,Juan Zhai,Chunrong Fang,Jin Song Dong,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FunPoison是一种保留功能完整性的数据集投毒方法，通过在代码执行路径中注入可编译的弱使用片段，仅污染10%的数据集即可实现高效防御，同时保持100%的编译通过率和功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模代码数据集的广泛可用性加速了代码大语言模型的发展，引发了对未授权数据集使用的担忧。数据集投毒提供了一种主动防御手段，但现有方法需要全量投毒且会破坏代码的可编译性。

Method: FunPoison通过三个核心模块实现功能保留的投毒：(1)可复用的语句级模板，结合自动修复和保守安全检查确保无副作用；(2)类型感知的合成模块，抑制静态分析警告并增强隐蔽性；(3)将短的可编译弱使用片段注入到代码执行路径中。

Result: 仅污染10%的数据集即可实现有效投毒，同时保持100%的编译通过率和功能正确性，对多种先进的代码净化技术具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FunPoison提出了一种功能保留的数据集投毒方法，在极低污染率下实现高效防御，为保护未授权代码数据集使用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The widespread availability of large-scale code datasets has accelerated the development of code large language models (CodeLLMs), raising concerns about unauthorized dataset usage. Dataset poisoning offers a proactive defense by reducing the utility of such unauthorized training. However, existing poisoning methods often require full dataset poisoning and introduce transformations that break code compilability. In this paper, we introduce FunPoison, a functionality-preserving poisoning approach that injects short, compilable weak-use fragments into executed code paths. FunPoison leverages reusable statement-level templates with automatic repair and conservative safety checking to ensure side-effect freedom, while a type-aware synthesis module suppresses static analysis warnings and enhances stealth. Extensive experiments show that FunPoison achieves effective poisoning by contaminating only 10% of the dataset, while maintaining 100% compilability and functional correctness, and remains robust against various advanced code sanitization techniques.

</details>


### [55] [Automation-Exploit: A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Adaptive Offensive Security with Digital Twin-Based Risk-Mitigated Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22427)
*Biagio Andreucci,Arcangelo Castiglione*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Automation-Exploit，一种全自主多智能体系统框架，用于复杂黑盒场景下的自适应攻防安全。该框架通过自主渗出可执行文件与上下文情报，结合逻辑与二进制攻击链，引入自适应安全架构缓解DoS风险，并采用条件同构验证处理内存损坏漏洞（通过数字孪生隔离调试）。在八个场景（含未公开零日环境）中验证了框架架构的鲁棒性，能在实际目标上执行风险缓解的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前攻防安全领域高度碎片化：企业平台因DoS风险规避内存损坏漏洞、AEG系统存在语义盲区、LLM智能体受安全对齐过滤和"实弹"执行危险限制。亟需一种能够弥合 reconnaissance 与 exploitation 之间抽象鸿沟的全自主框架。

Method: 提出Automation-Exploit多智能体框架：(1) 通过多协议自主渗出可执行文件与上下文情报；(2) 利用渗出数据驱动逻辑攻击链与二进制攻击链；(3) 引入自适应安全架构缓解DoS风险；(4) 对内存损坏漏洞采用条件同构验证：成功渗出目标二进制后动态实例化跨平台数字孪生，通过强制状态同步（libc对齐、运行时文件描述符钩子）在隔离副本中迭代调试高危载荷，实现物理目标上的"一次性"风险缓解执行。

Result: 在八个场景（含未公开零日环境以排除LLM数据污染）中验证框架的架构鲁棒性，证明其能防止"实弹"崩溃并在实际目标上执行风险缓解的利用。

Conclusion: Automation-Exploit框架成功弥合了攻防安全中侦察与利用之间的抽象鸿沟，通过多智能体协作、数字孪生隔离调试和自适应安全架构，实现了对复杂黑盒场景（含零日漏洞）的自主、低风险利用。

Abstract: The offensive security landscape is highly fragmented: enterprise platforms avoid memory-corruption vulnerabilities due to Denial of Service (DoS) risks, Automatic Exploit Generation (AEG) systems suffer from semantic blindness, and Large Language Model (LLM) agents face safety alignment filters and "Live Fire" execution hazards. We introduce Automation-Exploit, a fully autonomous Multi-Agent System (MAS) framework designed for adaptive offensive security in complex black-box scenarios. It bridges the abstraction gap between reconnaissance and exploitation by autonomously exfiltrating executables and contextual intelligence across multiple protocols, using this data to fuel both logical and binary attack chains. The framework introduces an adaptive safety architecture to mitigate DoS risks. While it natively resolves logical and web-based vulnerabilities, it employs a conditional isomorphic validation for high-risk memory-corruption flaws: if the target binary is successfully exfiltrated, it dynamically instantiates a cross-platform digital twin. By enforcing strict state synchronization, including libc alignment and runtime file descriptor hooking, potentially destructive payloads are iteratively debugged in an isolated replica. This enables a highly risk-mitigated "one-shot" execution on the physical target. Empirical evaluations across eight scenarios, including undocumented zero-day environments to rule out LLM data contamination, validate the framework's architectural resilience, demonstrating its ability to prevent "live fire" crashes and execute risk-mitigated compromises on actual targets.

</details>


### [56] [Horizontal SCA Attacks on Binary kP Algorithms using Chevallier-Mames Atomic Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22429)
*Gerald Isheanesu Matungamire,Alkistis Aikaterini Sigourou,Gerrit Schrock,Zoya Dyka,Peter Langendoerfer,Ievgen Kabin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明使用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式的椭圆曲线标量乘法(kP)算法（包括二进制右到左和左到右算法，以及带投影坐标随机化的左到右算法）仍易受单迹线侧信道分析攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆曲线密码系统中的标量乘法kP是最常被攻击的操作，尽管已有原子性原则和原子块模式来防御单迹线SCA攻击，但需要验证这些防御措施的有效性。

Method: 通过软件和硬件实现，对使用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式的二进制右到左和左到右kP算法进行单迹线SCA攻击测试，包括带投影坐标随机化的左到右算法。

Result: 所有测试的kP算法实现（包括使用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式的右到左、左到右算法，以及带投影坐标随机化的左到右算法）均未能抵御单迹线SCA攻击。

Conclusion: Chevallier-Mames原子块模式不足以保护kP算法免受单迹线SCA攻击，需要额外的防御措施。

Abstract: Scalar multiplication kP is the operation most frequently targeted in Elliptic Curve (EC) cryptosystems. To protect against single-trace Side-Channel Analysis (SCA) attacks, the atomicity principle and various atomic block patterns have been proposed in the past. In this work we use our software and hardware implementations to demonstrate that binary right-to left and left-to-right kP algorithms, when implemented with Chevallier-Mames atomic block patterns, are still vulnerable to single-trace SCA attacks. The vulnerability remains true for the left-to-right kP algorithm with projective coordinate randomization.

</details>


### [57] [SSG: Logit-Balanced Vocabulary Partitioning for LLM Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22438)
*Chenxi Gu,Xiaoning Du,John Grundy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对KGW水印方案在低熵场景（代码生成、数学推理）下效果退化的问题，提出SSG（Sort-then-Split by Groups）方法，通过将词表重分区为两个对数几率平衡的子集，提升每个token预测的水印强度下界，从而改善水印可检测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KGW水印方案在低熵设置（如代码生成和数学推理）下效果显著下降，因为随机词表分区导致水印强度下界过低，影响水印可检测性。

Method: 提出SSG（Sort-then-Split by Groups）方法，重新设计词表分区算法，将词表划分为两个对数几率（logit）平衡的子集，从而提升每个token预测的水印强度下界。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理数据集上的实验证明了SSG的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过改进词表分区策略而非修改水印嵌入机制本身，可以有效提升低熵场景下的水印可检测性。

Abstract: Watermarking has emerged as a promising technique for tracing the authorship of content generated by large language models (LLMs). Among existing approaches, the KGW scheme is particularly attractive due to its versatility, efficiency, and effectiveness in natural language generation. However, KGW's effectiveness degrades significantly under low-entropy settings such as code generation and mathematical reasoning. A crucial step in the KGW method is random vocabulary partitioning, which enables adjustments to token selection based on specific preferences. Our study revealed that the next-token probability distribution plays an critical role in determining how much, or even whether, we can modify token selection and, consequently, the effectiveness of watermarking. We refer to this characteristic, associated with the probability distribution of each token prediction, as \emph{watermark strength.} In cases of random vocabulary partitioning, the lower bound of watermark strength is dictated by the next-token probability distribution. However, we found that, by redesigning the vocabulary partitioning algorithm, we can potentially raise this lower bound. In this paper, we propose SSG (\textbf{S}ort-then-\textbf{S}plit by \textbf{G}roups), a method that partitions the vocabulary into two logit-balanced subsets. This design lifts the lower bound of watermark strength for each token prediction, thereby improving watermark detectability. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SSG.

</details>


### [58] [Information-Theoretic Authenticated PIR: From PIR-RV To APIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22505)
*Pengzhen Ke,Yuxuan Qin,Liang Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息论安全认证私密信息检索（itAPIR）的严格安全定义，证明了itPIR-RV方案可直接升级为itAPIR且无额外开销，填补了理论空白，支持抗量子恶意服务器环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证PIR方案依赖计算困难假设，而信息论PIR结果验证方案能实现完整性但查询隐私较弱且无法抵抗选择性失败攻击。本文旨在构建无条件安全的itAPIR，弥补理论缺口。

Method: 提出了itAPIR的严格信息论安全定义（统计隐私+完整性形式化）；建立了itAPIR与itPIR-RV的层次关系；证明了转换定理：有效itPIR-RV方案可直接升级为安全itAPIR且无额外开销。

Result: itPIR-RV方案可零额外开销升级为itAPIR，实现了无条件安全下的抗选择性失败攻击和查询隐私保护。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了itAPIR的理论空白，简化了方案设计，为恶意服务器环境下的抗量子PIR提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Private Information Retrieval (PIR) allows clients to retrieve database entries without leaking retrieval indices, yet malicious servers seriously compromise retrieval correctness. Existing Authenticated PIR (APIR) schemes resist selective-failure attacks but rely on computational hardness assumptions. In contrast, information-theoretic PIR with Result Verification (itPIR-RV) achieves integrity without computational assumptions, yet only provides relaxed query privacy with no defense against selective-failure attacks. This paper focuses on unconditionally secure information-theoretic APIR (itAPIR) constructions. We propose the rigorous information-theoretic security definition for itAPIR with statistical privacy against selective-failure attacks and integrity as core properties, formalize the hierarchical relation between itAPIR and itPIR-RV as a relaxed variant with identical integrity but basic query privacy, and prove a conversion theorem that valid itPIR-RV schemes can be directly upgraded to secure itAPIR with no extra overhead. Our work bridges the theoretical gap, simplifies itAPIR design, and enables quantum-resistant PIR in malicious server environments.

</details>


### [59] [ArmSSL: Adversarial Robust Black-Box Watermarking for Self-Supervised Learning Pre-trained Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22550)
*Yongqi Jiang,Yansong Gao,Boyu Kuang,Chunyi Zhou,Anmin Fu,Liquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ArmSSL是一个自监督学习模型水印框架，通过配对差异扩大、潜在表示纠缠和分布对齐等技术，实现了黑盒可验证性、对抗鲁棒性和效用保持的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL水印方法无法同时满足两个实际需求：(1) 在被盗编码器用于下游任务时，提供黑盒场景下的所有权验证能力；(2) 抵抗对抗性水印检测或移除，因为水印样本形成了可区分的分布外(OOD)聚类。

Method: ArmSSL包含三个关键技术：1) 配对差异扩大：强制清洁样本与其水印对应物在特征空间正交，产生可靠的黑盒验证信号；2) 潜在表示纠缠与分布对齐：前者将水印表示与清洁表示（非源类）纠缠以避免形成密集聚类，后者最小化水印与清洁表示的分布差异；3) 参考引导水印调优：将水印作为小型副任务学习，通过对齐水印编码器与原始清洁编码器在正常数据上的输出来保持效用。

Result: 在五个主流SSL框架和九个基准数据集上的大量实验以及与SOTA的端到端比较表明，ArmSSL实现了优越的所有权验证、可忽略的效用下降，以及针对各种对抗性检测和移除的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ArmSSL成功解决了SSL水印中黑盒可验证性与对抗鲁棒性的权衡问题，为自监督学习模型的知识产权保护提供了实用且安全的方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) encoders are invaluable intellectual property (IP). However, no existing SSL watermarking for IP protection can concurrently satisfy the following two practical requirements: (1) provide ownership verification capability under black-box suspect model access once the stolen encoders are used in downstream tasks; (2) be robust under adversarial watermark detection or removal, because the watermark samples form a distinguishable out-of-distribution (OOD) cluster. We propose ArmSSL, an SSL watermarking framework that assures black-box verifiability and adversarial robustness while preserving utility. For verification, we introduce paired discrepancy enlargement, enforcing feature-space orthogonality between the clean and its watermark counterpart to produce a reliable verification signal in black-box against the suspect model. For adversarial robustness, ArmSSL integrates latent representation entanglement and distribution alignment to suppress the OOD clustering. The former entangles watermark representations with clean representations (i.e., from non-source-class) to avoid forming a dense cluster of watermark samples, while the latter minimizes the distributional discrepancy between watermark and clean representations, thereby disguising watermark samples as natural in-distribution data. For utility, a reference-guided watermark tuning strategy is designed to allow the watermark to be learned as a small side task without affecting the main task by aligning the watermarked encoder's outputs with those of the original clean encoder on normal data. Extensive experiments across five mainstream SSL frameworks and nine benchmark datasets, along with end-to-end comparisons with SOTAs, demonstrate that ArmSSL achieves superior ownership verification, negligible utility degradation, and strong robustness against various adversarial detection and removal.

</details>


### [60] [Adversarial Co-Evolution of Malware and Detection Models: A Bilevel Optimization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22569)
*Olha Jurečková,Martin Jureček,Matouš Kozák,Róbert Lórencz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于双层优化的对抗性训练框架，通过建模攻防双方的迭代博弈过程，将恶意软件检测器的逃逸率从90%降至0-1.89%，并将攻击者的查询成本提升两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习的恶意软件检测器易受对抗样本攻击，传统的一次性对抗训练无法应对使用强化学习的自适应攻击者，亟需更鲁棒的防御框架。

Method: 提出基于双层优化的鲁棒防御框架，显式建模防御者与攻击者之间的策略交互，将其形式化为对抗性协同演化过程，并在MAB-malware框架下对Mokes、Strab、DCRat三个恶意软件家族进行评估。

Result: 标准分类器和基础对抗重训练的逃逸率高达90%，而双层优化方法将逃逸率降至0-1.89%；同时将攻击者的查询复杂度平均提升两个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过双层优化建模攻防迭代循环，对于构建能抵御不断演化的对抗性威胁的弹性恶意软件检测系统至关重要。

Abstract: Machine learning-based malware detectors are increasingly vulnerable to adversarial examples. Traditional defenses, such as one-shot adversarial training, often fail against adaptive attackers who use reinforcement learning to bypass detection. This paper proposes a robust defense framework based on bilevel optimization, explicitly modeling the strategic interaction between a defender and an attacker as an adversarial co-evolutionary process. We evaluate our approach using the MAB-malware framework against three distinct malware families: Mokes, Strab, and DCRat. Our experimental results demonstrate that while standard classifiers and basic adversarial retraining often remain vulnerable, showing evasion rates as high as 90 %, the proposed bilevel optimization approach consistently achieves near-total immunity, reducing evasion rates to 0 - 1.89 %. Furthermore, the iterative framework significantly increases the attacker's query complexity, raising the average cost of successful evasion by up to two orders of magnitude. These findings suggest that modeling the iterative cycle of attack and defense through bilevel optimization is essential for developing resilient malware detection systems capable of withstanding evolving adversarial threats.

</details>


### [61] [Detecting Concept Drift in Evolving Malware Families Using Rule-Based Classifier Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22629)
*Tomáš Kalný,Martin Jureček,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策树规则集的结构化概念漂移检测方法，在EMBER2024数据集上通过特征重要性、预测一致性、激活稳定性和覆盖度等指标量化漂移。实验表明，固定两月窗口结合特征级Pearson相关是最可靠的配置，且不同方法在不同恶意软件家族对中表现互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件分类中的概念漂移检测方法多依赖准确率退化等间接指标，缺乏对模型决策结构变化的直接量化分析，且未能系统比较不同时间窗口策略和度量方式的有效性。

Method: 使用决策树规则集提取结构化表示，在时间窗口上训练分类器，通过特征重要性、预测一致性、激活稳定性和覆盖度四种指标量化漂移，采用固定间隔和聚类两种窗口策略，在六类恶意软件的家族-良性及家族-家族场景下评估，并与RIPPER和Transcendent基线对比。

Result: 固定两月窗口结合特征级Pearson相关是最可靠配置，所有家族对在该配置下均产生正漂移-准确率相关；无单一方法在所有场景中占优，各方法互补。

Conclusion: 基于决策树规则集的结构化漂移检测方法有效，不同配置和方法在不同场景下各有优势，需结合使用以全面检测概念漂移。

Abstract: This work proposes a structural approach to concept drift detection in malware classification using decision tree rulesets. Classifiers are trained across temporal windows on the EMBER2024 dataset, and drift is quantified by comparing extracted rule representations using feature importance, prediction agreement, activation stability, and coverage metrics. These metrics are correlated with both accuracy degradation and data distribution shift as complementary drift indicators. The approach is evaluated across six malware families using fixed-interval and clustering-based windowing in family-vs-benign and family-vs-family settings, and compared against RIPPER and Transcendent baselines. Results show that fixed two-month windowing with feature-level Pearson correlation is the most reliable configuration, being the only one where all family pairs produce positive drift-accuracy correlations. The methods are complementary - no single approach dominates across all pairs.

</details>


### [62] [Adversarial Malware Generation in Linux ELF Binaries via Semantic-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22639)
*Lukáš Hrdonka,Martin Jureček*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对Linux ELF格式文件，提出了一种新的对抗性恶意软件生成器，成功实现了67.74%的逃逸率，并使检测器置信度平均降低0.50。研究发现，使用良性文件中的字符串作为数据源是最有效的修改方式，且目标分类器对可执行文件任意位置的字符串都敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要集中在Windows PE文件的对抗性恶意软件生成与检测，但针对Linux ELF格式的研究工作极少，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 开发了一种新的对抗性恶意软件生成器，以MalConv作为目标分类器，使用良性文件中的字符串作为数据源进行修改，并通过多种指标进行系统评估。

Result: 生成器实现了67.74%的逃逸率，检测器置信度平均降低0.50。

Conclusion: 目标分类器对可执行文件任意位置的字符串均敏感；使用良性文件字符串进行修改是对抗性攻击的最有效策略。

Abstract: Malware development and detection have undergone significant changes in recent years as modern concepts, such as machine learning, have been used for both adversarial attacks and defense. Despite intensive research on Windows Portable Executable (PE) files, there is minimal work on Linux Executable and Linkable Format (ELF). In this work, we summarize the academic papers submitted in this field and develop a new adversarial malware generator for the ELF format. Using a variety of metrics, we thoroughly evaluated our generator and achieved an Evasion Rate of 67.74 % while changing the confidence of the malware detector by -0.50 in the mean case for the dataset used. In our approach, we chose MalConv as the target classifier. Using this classifier, we found that the most successful modifications used strings typical of benign files as a data source. We conducted a variety of experiments and concluded that the target classifier appears sensitive to strings at any location within the executable file.

</details>
