### [63] [Feedback Over Form: Why Execution Feedback Matters More Than Pipeline Topology in 1-3B Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21950)
*Charles Junichi McAndrews*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探究了1-3B小语言模型通过流水线组合（特别是带执行反馈的自优化循环）在代码生成任务上的性能提升。研究发现：执行反馈带来的自优化能显著修复运行时错误（如NameError、SyntaxError），但对逻辑错误（如AssertionError）改善有限；精炼器能力比生成器能力更重要；早期停止至关重要；代码专用模型优于通用模型流水线；进化搜索未发现比简单生成-执行-精炼循环更优的拓扑结构；单次评估的适应度会高估5-7%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（1-3B参数）便于本地部署，但在复杂代码生成任务上能力有限。研究旨在探索通过流水线组合多个小模型是否能恢复部分丢失的能力，以及流水线结构复杂度是否比简单的自优化循环更有优势。

Method: 使用1-3B参数模型构建代码生成流水线，引入执行反馈机制；采用受NEAT启发的进化搜索探索不同流水线拓扑结构；在HumanEval（164个问题）和sanitized MBPP（427个问题）上评估，全部在单个笔记本上本地推理。

Result: 自优化带来超过4个标准差的提升，主要修复运行时错误而非逻辑错误；1.5B生成器+3B精炼器组合媲美3B模型同时承担两角色；早期停止不可或缺，否则每轮迭代均为净负收益；代码专用模型优于所有通用模型流水线；进化搜索未发现优于简单循环的结构；单次评估适应度导致5-7%的性能膨胀。

Conclusion: 在1-3B模型规模下，执行反馈对代码生成的提升远大于流水线结构复杂度带来的增益；模型专业化比流水线架构更重要；进化搜索在此约束空间内未能发现更优的拓扑结构。

Abstract: Small language models (1-3B) are practical to run locally, but individually limited on harder code generation tasks. We ask whether composing them into pipelines can recover some of that lost capability. We study code generation pipelines built from 1-3B models with execution feedback, and use a NEAT-inspired evolutionary search to test whether more complex pipeline structure helps beyond a simple refinement loop. We evaluate on HumanEval (164 problems) and sanitized MBPP (427 problems), all with local inference on a single laptop. Self-refinement with execution feedback improves code generation by more than 4 standard deviations on both benchmarks. The gains are narrow in mechanism: refinement fixes many runtime errors (especially NameError and SyntaxError), but rarely fixes logic errors such as AssertionError. Within our tested general-purpose model pool, generator identity mattered less than refiner capability: a 1.5B generator paired with a 3B refiner matched a 3B model doing both roles. Early stopping is essential; without it, every iteration is net-negative. The code-specialized models outperform every general-purpose pipeline configuration, suggesting model specialization matters more than pipeline architecture. Preliminary text-only pipeline experiments without execution feedback did not show gains at this scale. In our constrained search space, evolutionary search mostly rediscovered the same simple generate-execute-refine loop we found manually, with no clearly significant gain from added topology. Single-evaluation fitness inflates results by 5-7 percent, selecting lucky genomes over good ones. On these benchmarks at 1-3B scale, execution feedback mattered more than added pipeline complexity in determining whether composition helped.

</details>


### [64] [Documentless Assessments Using Nominal Group Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22003)
*Eduardo Miranda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合用户故事与规划扑克技术的小组访谈方法，用于在无文档支持的流程评估中促进参与者协作，并在实际咨询案例中成功使分歧各方达成一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的流程评估（如CMMI评估）通常依赖文档审查和审计式访谈，但在缺乏文档支持的环境下难以开展，且评估过程中参与者之间容易产生分歧，缺乏协作。作者旨在设计一种不依赖文档的评估技术，同时促进参与者之间的沟通与共识。

Method: 该方法借鉴敏捷软件开发中的用户故事（User Stories）概念，将CMMI的具体实践以业务场景的语言具体化表达，替代传统的文档审查；同时引入规划扑克（Planning Poker）技术替代审计式访谈，用于事实发现和交叉验证。通过小组访谈的形式进行集体评估。

Result: 在一项实际咨询任务中成功应用，使得之前存在分歧的参与者能够相互沟通并就问题达成一致意见。

Conclusion: 所提出的小组访谈技术能够有效支持无文档流程评估，并在促进参与者协作方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: This paper describes a group interview technique designed to support documentless process assessments while promoting at the same time collaboration among assessment participants. The method was successfully used in one consulting assignment where it got previously discording participants, talking to each other and agreeing on the issues. The technique borrows from agile software development the concept of user stories to cast CMMIs specific practices in concrete terms and the Planning Poker technique, instead of document reviews and audit like interviews, for fact finding and corroboration.

</details>


### [65] [FlyCatcher: Neural Inference of Runtime Checkers from Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22028)
*Beatriz Souza,Chang Lou,Suman Nath,Michael Pradel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlyCatcher，一种从现有测试中自动推断运行时检查器的方法，用于检测复杂软件系统中的静默故障。该方法结合了大语言模型合成、静态分析和动态验证，推断出可维护影子状态的状态化检查器。在400个测试和四个复杂系统的评估中，FlyCatcher 推断出334个检查器（300个正确），相比现有方法多产生2.6倍正确的检查器，并能检测出5.2倍更多的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统常发生静默故障（不引发显式错误的语义违反），而编写系统特定的运行时检查器来检测这类错误虽有效但耗时费力，实践中很少进行。因此需要自动化方法来降低运行时检查器的构建成本。

Method: FlyCatcher 结合大语言模型（LLM）合成、静态分析和动态验证，从已有测试用例中自动推断运行时检查器。检查器监视特定方法调用并断言应满足的属性，通过维护影子状态（shadow state）来抽象系统实际状态，从而实现对系统行为的状态化推理。

Result: 在四个广泛使用的复杂软件系统的400个测试上评估，FlyCatcher 推断出334个检查器，其中300个经交叉验证正确。相比最先进方法，多产生2.6倍正确的检查器，并能检测5.2倍更多的错误。

Conclusion: FlyCatcher 通过自动化从测试中推断运行时检查器，促进了运行时检查在复杂软件系统中检测静默故障的广泛采用，证明了该方法是实用且有效的自动化检测方案。

Abstract: Complex software systems often suffer from silent failures, i.e., violations of the intended semantics that do not cause explicit errors. A promising approach to detect such errors is to use system-specific runtime checkers that monitor the execution of a system and check for violations of the intended semantics. However, writing such checkers for a given software system is challenging and time-consuming, and hence, rarely done in practice. This work presents FlyCatcher, an automated approach to derive runtime checkers from existing tests, i.e., from a resource available for most software systems. The critical challenge of such an approach is to generalize the behavioral properties encoded in a test case to arbitrary executions of a system. FlyCatcher addresses this challenge through a combination of LLM-based synthesis, static analysis, and dynamic validation, which infers a checker that monitors specific method calls and asserts properties that should hold when they are called. The inferred checkers are stateful, i.e., they reason about the system's behavior by maintaining a shadow state that abstracts the actual system state as needed by the checker. Our evaluation applies FlyCatcher to 400 tests from four widely used, complex software systems. The approach infers 334 checkers, out of which 300 are found to be correct via cross-validation. Compared with a state-of-the-art approach, our approach infers 2.6x more correct checkers, which enables it to detect 5.2x more errors. By contributing to the automated inference of runtime checkers from tests, this work enables the broader adoption of runtime checking as a practical approach to detect silent failures in complex software systems.

</details>


### [66] [Call-Chain-Aware LLM-Based Test Generation for Java Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22046)
*Guancheng Wang,Qinghua Xu,Lionel C. Briand,Zhaoqiang Guo,Kui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CAT是一种基于调用链感知的LLM测试生成方法，通过将调用链和依赖上下文显式纳入提示词，结合静态分析和迭代修复，在Defects4J和真实项目上相比SOTA方法PANTA在线覆盖率和分支覆盖率上分别提升18.04%和21.74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的单元测试生成方法主要依赖执行路径信息来构建提示词，但对于具有复杂类间依赖、深调用链和复杂对象初始化需求的软件系统而言，执行路径信息往往不足，导致生成的测试用例质量不高。

Method: CAT方法通过专用静态分析显式建模调用者-被调用者关系、对象构造函数和第三方依赖，将调用链和依赖上下文纳入提示词，并支持生成失败时的迭代测试修复。

Result: 在Defects4J基准上，CAT相比PANTA在线覆盖率和分支覆盖率分别提升18.04%和21.74%；在LLM截止日期后发布的四个真实GitHub项目上也持续取得更优性能；消融实验证明了调用链和依赖上下文的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式整合调用链和依赖上下文能显著提升LLM生成项目级单元测试的质量，验证了该方法在复杂软件系统上的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for generating project-level unit tests. However, existing state-of-the-art approaches primarily rely on execution-path information to guide prompt construction, which is often insufficient for complex software systems with rich inter-class dependencies, deep call chains, and intricate object initialization requirements. In this paper, we present CAT, a novel call-chain-aware LLM-based test generation approach that explicitly incorporates call-chain and dependency contexts into prompts through dedicated static analysis. To construct executable, semantically valid test contexts, CAT systematically models caller--callee relationships, object constructors, and third-party dependencies, and supports iterative test fixing when generation failures occur. We evaluate CAT on the widely used Defects4J benchmark and on four real-world GitHub projects released after the LLM's cut-off date. The results show that, across projects in Defects4J, CAT improves line and branch coverage by 18.04% and 21.74%, respectively, over the state-of-the-art approach PANTA, while consistently achieving superior performance on post-cutoff real-world projects. An ablation study further demonstrates the importance of call-chain and dependency contexts in CAT.

</details>


### [67] [TRACE: Topology-aware Reconstruction of Accidents in CARLA for AV Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22068)
*Nahian Salsabil,Sebastian Elbaum*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TRACE是一个自动化流水线，能将NHTSA事故报告重建为高保真CARLA仿真场景，生成了包含52个多样化事故场景的基准测试集，用于测试自动驾驶系统应对真实世界故障的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶验证基准要么生成合成冲突，要么将事故描述映射到抽象道路几何，无法捕捉真实世界碰撞的拓扑复杂性。需要一种能从真实事故报告中自动化重建高保真仿真场景的方法。

Method: (1)检索OpenStreetMap数据保留精确道路拓扑；(2)利用大语言模型从道路几何和碰撞前操作推断车辆初始状态；(3)从半结构化报告数据生成仿真轨迹。

Result: 构建了包含52个多样化事故场景的基准，涵盖多种碰撞类型、道路拓扑和碰撞前操作。

Conclusion: TRACE提供了一种自动化真实事故场景重建的方法，产出的基准可作为测试自动驾驶系统对抗真实世界故障的挑战性开源资源。

Abstract: Validating Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) requires exposure to rare, safety-critical scenarios, infrequent in routine driving data. Existing benchmarks address this by generating synthetic conflicts or mapping accident descriptions to abstract road geometries, failing to capture the topological complexity of real-world crashes. We introduce TRACE , a pipeline that automates the reconstruction of NHTSA crash reports into high-fidelity CARLA simulations by (1) retrieving site-specific OpenStreetMap data to preserve exact road topology, (2) leveraging Large Language Models to infer vehicles' initial state from road geometry and pre-crash maneuvers, and (3) generating simulation trajectories from semi-structured report data. Using this pipeline, we curated a benchmark of 52 diverse accident scenarios covering varied collision types, road topologies, and pre-crash maneuvers, providing a challenging open source resource for testing AV systems against real-world failures.

</details>


### [68] [Ethics Testing: Proactive Identification of Generative AI System Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22089)
*Shin Hwei Tan,Haibo Wang,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"伦理测试"概念，用于系统识别生成式AI系统（如ChatGPT）自动生成内容中的软件危害（如有害行为或侵犯知识产权行为），并通过五个案例研究展示其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如公平性测试）无法系统识别生成式AI系统自动生成内容中的软件危害，缺乏针对不道德行为（如有害内容、侵犯知识产权）的测试方法。

Method: 提出"伦理测试"概念，定义其目标为系统检测自动生成内容中因不道德行为导致的软件危害，并通过五个案例研究展示实施方式。

Result: 通过五个案例研究证明伦理测试能够有效识别生成式AI系统中的软件危害。

Conclusion: 伦理测试为生成式AI系统提供了一种新的系统化测试方法论，填补了现有测试方法在检测不道德行为相关危害方面的空白。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GAI) systems that can automatically generate content in the form of source code or other contents (e.g., images) has seen increasing popularity due to the emergence of tools such as ChatGPT which rely on Large Language Models (LLMs). Misuse of the automatically generated content can incur serious consequences due to potential harms in the generated content. Despite the importance of ensuring the quality of automatically generated content, there is little to no approach that can systematically generate tests for identifying software harms in the content generated by these GAI systems. In this article, we introduce the novel concept of ethics testing which aims to systematically generate tests for identifying software harms. Different from existing testing methodologies (e.g., fairness testing that aims to identifying software discrimination), ethics testing aims to systematically detect software harms that could be induced due to unethical behavior (e.g., harmful behavior or behavior that violates intellectual property rights) in automatically generated content. We introduced the concept of ethics testing, discussed the challenges therewithin, and conducted five case studies to show how ethics testing can be performed for generative AI systems.

</details>


### [69] [Evaluating LLM-Based Goal Extraction in Requirements Engineering: Prompting Strategies and Their Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22207)
*Anna Arnaudo,Riccardo Coppola,Maurizio Morisio,Flavio Giobergia,Andrea Bioddo,Angelo Bongiorno,Luca Dadone*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型链（Chain of LLMs）的三阶段流水线方法，用于从软件文档中自动化提取功能目标（参与者识别、高层目标提取、低层目标提取）。实验表明该流水线在低层目标识别上达到61%准确率，更适合作为加速人工提取的工具而非完全替代方案。反馈循环机制结合零样本学习优于独立的小样本学习，但反馈与小样本学习结合无额外优势，表明性能瓶颈在于"评论者"LLM的提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程中的目标导向需求工程（GORE）过程通常具有文本性、重复性特征，人工处理效率低下，而大语言模型在自动化生成和处理RE制品方面展现出潜力。本文旨在探索利用LLM链自动化GORE过程的可行性与局限性。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：1）参与者识别；2）高层目标提取；3）低层目标提取。核心机制包括：LLM链（chain of LLMs）、工程化提示（engineered prompts）、上下文学习（in-context learning）变体、生成-评论者机制（generation-critic mechanism，即两个LLM之间的反馈循环），并通过消融实验评估各组件贡献。

Result: 低层目标识别准确率为61%；反馈循环+零样本学习优于独立小样本学习；消融实验表明无反馈循环时性能略有下降；反馈机制与小样本学习结合无优势，提示策略是评论者LLM的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该方法更适合作为加速人工提取的工具而非完全替代；未来研究方向包括优化小样本示例的数量与质量、集成检索增强生成（RAG）和思维链（CoT）提示以提升准确率。

Abstract: Due to the textual and repetitive nature of many Requirements Engineering (RE) artefacts, Large Language Models (LLMs) have proven useful to automate their generation and processing. In this paper, we discuss a possible approach for automating the Goal-Oriented Requirements Engineering (GORE) process by extracting functional goals from software documentation through three phases: actor identification, high and low-level goal extraction. To implement these functionalities, we propose a chain of LLMs fed with engineered prompts. We experimented with different variants of in-context learning and measured the similarities between input data and in-context examples to better investigate their impact. Another key element is the generation-critic mechanism, implemented as a feedback loop involving two LLMs. Although the pipeline achieved 61% accuracy in low-level goal identification, the final stage, these results indicate the approach is best suited as a tool to accelerate manual extraction rather than as a full replacement. The feedback-loop mechanism with Zero-shot outperformed stand-alone Few-shot, with an ablation study suggesting that performance slightly degrades without the feedback cycle. However, we reported that the combination of the feedback mechanism with Few-shot does not deliver any advantage, possibly suggesting that the primary performance ceiling is the prompting strategy applied to the 'critic' LLM. Together with the refinement of both the quantity and quality of the Shot examples, future research will integrate Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting to improve accuracy.

</details>


### [70] [RAG-Reflect: Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Reflections for Comment-Driven Code Maintenance on Stack Overflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22217)
*Mehedi Hasan Shanto,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Alioune Ngom*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG-Reflect框架，通过检索增强推理和自我反思机制，在不进行任务特定微调的情况下，实现了与微调模型相当的评论-编辑有效性预测性能（F1=0.78），在SOUP基准上超越传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线编程平台（如Stack Overflow）上大量用户评论包含感谢或澄清内容，仅少部分评论触发实际代码编辑。现有方法需要任务特定训练或简单提示技术，难以准确识别真正引发代码变更的有效评论。

Method: RAG-Reflect框架采用三阶段运行时工作流：初始化阶段通过解释模块分析知识库生成验证规则；推理阶段依次进行(1)检索上下文示例、(2)推理评论-编辑因果关系、(3)利用预置规则进行决策反思。框架基于一次性模式分析阶段构建，集成大语言模型与检索增强推理及自我反思机制。

Result: 在SOUP基准上，Precision=0.81, Recall=0.74, F1=0.78；优于Logistic Regression、XGBoost及多种提示技术等传统基线；接近微调模型性能（F1=0.773）且无需重新训练；消融实验表明检索和反思模块均显著提升性能。

Conclusion: RAG-Reflect证明智能体AI原则（检索增强推理+自我反思）可在无需任务特定训练的情况下实现微调级别性能，为软件维护任务提供高效模块化解决方案。

Abstract: User comments on online programming platforms such as Stack Overflow play a vital role in maintaining the correctness and relevance of shared code examples. However, the majority of comments express gratitude or clarification, while only a small fraction highlight actionable issues that drive meaningful edits. This paper demonstrates how agentic AI principles can revolutionize software maintenance tasks by presenting RAG-Reflect, a modular framework that achieves fine-tuned-level performance for valid comment-edit prediction without task-specific training. Valid Comment-Edit Prediction (VCP) is the task of determining whether a user comment directly triggered a subsequent code edit. The framework integrates large language models (LLMs) with retrieval-augmented reasoning and self-reflection mechanisms. RAG-Reflect operates through a three-stage runtime workflow built on a one-time pattern analysis phase. During initialization, an Interpretation module analyzes the knowledge base to generate validation rules. At inference time, the system (1) retrieves contextual examples, (2) reasons about comment-edit causality, and (3) reflects on decisions using the pre-established rules. We evaluate RAG-Reflect on the publicly available SOUP benchmark, achieving Precision = 0.81, Recall = 0.74, and F1 = 0.78, outperforming traditional baselines (e.g., Logistic Regression, XGBoost, different prompting techniques) and closely approaching the performance of fine-tuned models (F1 = 0.773) without retraining. Our ablation and stage-level analyses show that both retrieval and reflection modules substantially enhance performance.

</details>


### [71] [Enhancing a gamified tool for UML modeling education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22400)
*Giacomo Garaccione,Riccardo Coppola,Luca Ardito*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了UMLegend工具从仅支持类图扩展到支持用例图的过程，通过重构模块化架构以适配多种建模语言，并计划在2026年进行纵向研究评估长期游戏化学习对学生UML建模能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生普遍低估UML用例图和类图在软件工程教育中的重要性，现有游戏化工具几乎仅关注类图，缺乏对其他建模符号（如用例图）的支持。

Method: 基于2025年UMLegend工具实验的结果进行增强：重构为模块化架构以支持多种建模语言，新增用例图练习类型，保留动态反馈和长期游戏化机制。

Result: 实现了从单一类图支持到多建模语言支持的扩展，工具架构重构为模块化设计，便于未来添加更多软件工程主题和建模符号。

Conclusion: 新版UMLegend预期能提升学生对多种UML建模符号的学习效果，计划在2026年开展纵向研究验证长期游戏化UML教学对学生成绩的改善作用。

Abstract: Unified Modeling Language (UML) Use Case and Class Diagrams are fundamental modeling notations in Software Engineering (SE) education due to their importance for requirements and model-based engineering, yet their relevance is underestimated by students, who tend to dismiss the topic as secondary. Gamification has been adopted to make modeling education more appealing, but existing tools focus almost exclusively on class diagrams, leaving support for use cases and other notations unexplored. In 2025, we designed UMLegend, a gamified tool for class diagrams that offered dynamic feedback to help students learn correct modeling practices and multiple long-term mechanics to increase engagement, and performed a study with the tool. With this paper, we describe how we enhanced UMLegend following the results of the experiment so that it can support more modeling languages, with use case diagrams being added to the type of available exercises in the tool. The revised version has been refactored to have a modular architecture, to make it easier to add other software engineering topics and additional modeling notations. We also describe the potential impact we expect the new version to have, and outline a longitudinal study we intend to perform in 2026 where we will assess whether long-term UML gamification leads to improved student performance.

</details>


### [72] [Gamifying Architectural Governance to Reduce Organizational Coupling in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22454)
*Xiaozhou Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出通过游戏化方法（积分、徽章、排行榜、架构改进任务）对微服务系统中的组织耦合（OC）进行行为驱动治理，通过持续挖掘仓库数据检测架构边界违规，以激励开发者维护服务边界并提升架构可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构实践中，开发者频繁跨服务贡献导致组织耦合（OC），逐渐侵蚀架构边界并增加协调开销，需要一种行为驱动的方法来影响开发者行为、减少OC。

Method: 提出游戏化治理框架：持续挖掘仓库数据检测架构边界违规和依赖增长，将这些信号转化为游戏化设计（积分、徽章、排行榜、架构改进任务），并结合仓库挖掘、架构度量和游戏化机制。

Result: 给出了概念框架和评估路线图，旨在评估游戏化OC治理对开发者参与度的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了软件架构治理、社会技术分析和游戏化交叉领域的新研究方向，展示了行为激励在支持可持续微服务演化方面的潜力。

Abstract: Microservice is a popular software architecture that relies on decentralized teams and clear service ownership to support modularity and scalability. However, in practice, developers frequently contribute across multiple services, creating organizational coupling (OC) that gradually erodes architectural boundaries and increases coordination overhead. This study proposes a vision for behavior-driven architectural governance through gamification in microservice systems to influence developer behavior and reduce OC. Our approach introduces a gamified framework that continuously mines repository data to detect architectural boundary violations and increasing service dependencies, and translates those signals into gameful designs, including points, badges, leaderboards, and architecture improvement quests. We outline a conceptual framework that integrates repository mining, architectural metrics, and gamification mechanisms to encourage developers to maintain service boundaries and improve architectural maintainability. Furthermore, we present an evaluation roadmap to assess the impact of gamified OC governance and developer engagement. This work aims to open a new research direction at the intersection of software architecture governance, socio-technical analysis, and gamification, highlighting the potential of behavioral incentives to support sustainable microservice evolution.

</details>


### [73] [Test Design and Review Argumentation in AI-Assisted Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22473)
*Eduard Paul Enoiu,Robert Feldt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对AI辅助测试生成中缺乏设计决策论证表示的问题，提出了一个概念性分类法和结构化模板，通过测试目标、主张、理由和证据四个要素描述测试用例，支持测试设计的构建性和回顾性使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作聚焦于分类测试技术、将需求与测试关联以及构建系统保证论证，但未明确表示单个测试设计决策背后的论证过程。随着AI生成测试用例的能力增强，理解生成物的存在理由和支撑依据成为新挑战。

Method: 提出一个概念性分类法和结构化模板，将测试用例表征为四个要素：测试目标（test goal）、主张（claim）、理由（reason）和证据（evidence）。

Result: 该分类法既可用于测试设计的构建性用途（指导生成过程），也可用于评审时的回顾性用途（评估附加论证的质量，而非判断测试用例本身的合理性或客观价值）。

Conclusion: 提供了一个显式表示测试设计决策论证的框架，弥补了现有工作在单个测试用例论证表示方面的空白。

Abstract: AI assistants can increasingly generate and evolve test cases. The challenge is no longer merely to produce them, but also to help engineers understand why a generated artefact exists and what supports it. Existing work has focused on classifying testing techniques, linking requirements to tests and structuring system assurance arguments, but it does not explicitly represent the argumentation behind individual test design decisions. We propose a conceptual taxonomy and a structured template for AI-assisted test generation that characterizes a test case by its test goal, claim, reason, and evidence. The taxonomy is intended for both constructive use during test design and retrospective use during review, to assess the quality of the attached argument rather than the plausibility or objective value of the generated test cases.

</details>


### [74] [A Comparison of ROS 2 and AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform Against Industry-Elicited Automotive Middleware Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22576)
*Lucas Hegerath,David Philipp Klüner,Philipp Pelcz,Viswanatha Reddy Batchu,Marius Molz,Julius Kahle,Thomas Schulik,Stefan Kowalewski,Alexandru Kampmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文比较了ROS 2 Jazzy和AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform R24-11两种中间件框架，基于ZF集团汽车软件工程师提出的实际需求，提供了工业视角下的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在软件定义汽车中，中间件是软件组件间通信、集成和协调的基础。然而，从工业实践中获取中间件框架的实际需求评估视角较为困难，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从主流汽车供应商ZF集团收集汽车软件工程师的实际需求，然后评估ROS 2 Jazzy和AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform R24-11这两种流行中间件框架对这些需求的满足程度。

Result: 提供了两种中间件框架在实际工业需求下的对比评估结果，揭示了各自的优势和不足。

Conclusion: 为汽车中间件的开发和评估提供了清晰的优先级理解，有助于指导未来中间件的选择和发展方向。

Abstract: In software-defined vehicles, automotive middleware plays a fundamental role in enabling efficient communication, integration, and coordination among software components. This paper examines how well two of the currently most popular middleware frameworks, ROS 2 Jazzy and AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform R24-11, meet practical requirements elicited from automotive software engineers at one of the major automotive supplier companies, ZF Group. Our objective is to provide insight into an otherwise difficult-to-obtain industrial perspective and support a clearer understanding of priorities in the development and evaluation of middleware for automotive applications.

</details>


### [75] [From Natural Language to Verified Code: Toward AI Assisted Problem-to-Code Generation with Dafny-Based Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22601)
*Md Erfan,Md Kamal Hossain Chowdhury,Ahmed Ryan,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出NL2VC-60数据集，评估7个开源LLM在形式化验证代码生成上的表现，发现通过签名提示和自愈反馈策略，Gemma 4-31B达到90.91%的验证成功率，证明开源LLM也能有效进行高可信软件开发的代码合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码常包含错误或幻觉代码，形式化验证要求模型同步生成实现逻辑和形式化规约并通过验证器证明，但从自然语言到形式化规约的转换极具挑战。

Method: 构建NL2VC-60数据集（60个复杂算法问题），对7个开源LLM采用三级提示策略：无上下文提示、签名提示（结构锚点）、自愈提示（Dafny验证器迭代反馈），并集成uDebug平台防止空洞验证。

Result: 无上下文提示几乎全部失败；签名提示和自愈提示显著提升性能：Gemma 4-31B达90.91%验证成功率，GPT-OSS 120B从0%提升至81.82%。

Conclusion: 形式化验证对于开源LLM是可行的，它们可作为有效的辅助工具合成复杂注释，推动高可信软件开发。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise in automated software engineering, yet their guarantee of correctness is frequently undermined by erroneous or hallucinated code. To enforce model honesty, formal verification requires LLMs to synthesize implementation logic alongside formal specifications that are subsequently proven correct by a mathematical verifier. However, the transition from informal natural language to precise formal specification remains an arduous task. Our work addresses this by providing the NaturalLanguage2VerifiedCode (NL2VC)-60 dataset: a collection of 60 complex algorithmic problems. We evaluate 11 randomly selected problem sets across seven open-weight LLMs using a tiered prompting strategy: contextless prompts, signature prompts providing structural anchors, and self-healing prompts utilizing iterative feedback from the Dafny verifier. To address vacuous verification, where models satisfy verifiers with trivial specifications, we integrate the uDebug platform to ensure functional validation. Our results show that while contextless prompting leads to near-universal failure, structural signatures and iterative self-healing facilitate a dramatic performance turnaround. Specifically, Gemma 4-31B achieved a 90.91\% verification success rate, while GPT-OSS 120B rose from zero to 81.82\% success with signature-guided feedback. These findings indicate that formal verification is now attainable for open-weight LLMs, which serve as effective apprentices for synthesizing complex annotations and facilitating high-assurance software development.

</details>


### [76] [Quality-Driven Selective Mutation for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22640)
*Zaheed Ahmed,Emmanuel Charleson Dapaah,Philip Makedonski,Jens Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概率框架，从抵抗性（难杀死）和现实性（模拟真实故障）两个维度量化深度学习变异体的质量，实验表明质量驱动选择可在减少55.6%变异体数量的同时保持有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习领域的变异测试缺乏一个统一的度量标准来同时衡量变异体的抵抗性（难杀死程度）和现实性（模拟真实故障的能力），导致选择性变异缺乏有效指导。

Method: 提出一个概率框架，抵抗性使用统计杀死概率来衡量，现实性使用广义Jaccard相似度衡量变异体与真实故障的可检测性模式相似度。在四个真实深度学习故障数据集上评估，三个用于选择和估计，一个用于验证。

Result: 质量驱动的选择可在基线对齐的选择阈值下，减少高达55.6%的变异体生成数量，同时保持典型的抵抗性和现实性水平。

Conclusion: 双目标选择可以在降低成本的同时，不损害变异体在测试改进或故障模拟任一角色中的实用性。

Abstract: Mutants support testing and debugging in two roles: (i) as test goals and (ii) as substitutes for real faults. Hard-to-kill mutants provide better guidance for test improvement, while realism is essential when mutants are used to simulate real bugs. Building on these roles, selective mutation for deep learning (DL) aims to reduce the cost of mutant generation and execution by choosing operator configurations that yield resistant and realistic mutants. However, the DL literature lacks a unified measure that captures both aspects. This study presents a probabilistic framework to quantify mutant quality along two complementary axes: resistance and realism. Resistance adapts the classical notion of hard-to-kill mutants to the DL setting using statistical killing probabilities, while realism is measured via the generalized Jaccard similarity between mutant and real-fault detectability patterns. The framework enables ranking and filtering of low-quality mutation-operator configurations without assuming a specific use case. We empirically evaluate the approach on four datasets of real DL faults. Three datasets (CleanML, DeepFD, and DeepLocalize) are used to estimate and select high-quality operator configurations, and the held-out defect4ML dataset is used for validation. Results show that quality-driven selection reduces the number of generated mutants by up to 55.6% while preserving typical levels of resistance and realism under baseline-aligned selection thresholds. These findings confirm that dual-objective selection can lower cost without compromising the usefulness of mutants for either role.

</details>


### [77] [Verifier Warnings Do Not Improve Comprehensibility Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22653)
*Nadeeshan De Silva,Martin Kellogg,Oscar Chaparro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文探究了将验证器警告总数作为特征加入机器学习模型是否能提升代码可理解性预测性能。实验发现，加入该特征与仅使用语法和开发者特征的模型相比，预测性能无显著差异，表明验证器警告总数虽与可理解性相关但判别力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于先前元分析发现代码验证器警告总数与人类代码可理解性之间存在相关性，研究者猜想将该语义特征加入机器学习模型可提升代码可理解性预测性能。

Method: 采用控制-实验设计，将验证器警告总数作为额外特征加入现有机器学习模型，并与仅使用语法和开发者特征的基线模型进行性能对比分析。

Result: 包含与不包含验证器警告总数特征的模型在代码可理解性预测性能上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 验证器警告总数虽与代码可理解性存在相关性，但其判别力有限；结合语法和开发者特征已足以有效预测人类判断的代码可理解性，无需额外引入该语义特征。

Abstract: Proponents of software verification suggest that code simplicity is linked to the effort to verify code, hypothesizing that formal verifiers produce fewer false positive warnings and require less manual intervention when analyzing simpler code. A recent meta-analysis study found empirical support for this hypothesis: a small correlation between the sum of verifier warnings and human-derived code comprehensibility metrics. Based on this finding, we conjectured that using the sum of verifier tool (verifier) warnings to represent program semantic information as an input feature to machine learning (ML) models for code comprehensibility prediction can enhance their performance, when combined with traditional syntactic and developer features.
  To test this conjecture, we performed a control-treatment experiment incorporating the verifier warning sum feature into machine learning models from the literature, and conducted a comparative analysis of their performance against models trained only on syntactic and developer features. We found no significant difference in the prediction performance of models with and without the warnings feature. Our findings suggest that while a correlation exists, the verifier warning sum offers limited discriminative power: combining syntactic and developer features is just as effective for predicting human-judged code comprehensibility.

</details>


### [78] [RealBench: A Repo-Level Code Generation Benchmark Aligned with Real-World Software Development Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22659)
*Jia Li,Hongyi Deng,Yiran Zhang,Kechi Zhang,Tianqi Shao,Tiankuo Zhao,Weinan Wang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li,Yang Liu,Yingtao Fang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RealBench是一个面向仓库级代码生成的新基准，其设计贴合真实工业软件开发实践（包含自然语言需求+UML图设计）。实验发现：LLM在仓库级代码生成上表现远差于单函数生成，模型间差距显著；LLM擅长按UML定位创建模块但生成质量差（语法/逻辑错误）；小仓库适合全量生成，复杂仓库适合逐模块生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准（如HumanEval）仅基于自然语言需求评估，与企业中基于结构化设计文档（如UML图）进行开发的真实实践存在脱节，导致基准分数无法真实反映代码生成在软件开发自动化中的实际帮助程度。

Method: 构建RealBench基准，每个样本包含自然语言需求+UML图（系统设计），模拟真实开发场景。基于该基准对多个先进LLM进行仓库级代码生成能力系统评估，并比较不同生成策略（全量生成 vs. 逐模块生成）。

Result: 1) 仓库级生成任务上LLM性能远低于单函数级基准，且模型间差距显著；2) LLM能较好定位UML中定义的模块，但生成模块因语法和逻辑错误质量差；3) 小仓库全量生成最优，复杂仓库逐模块生成更优。

Conclusion: 现有基准高估了LLM在真实软件工程场景中的代码生成能力，RealBench更贴近实际需求，揭示LLM在结构化设计理解和高质量仓库级代码生成方面仍有显著不足。

Abstract: Writing code requires significant time and effort in software development. To automate this process, researchers have made substantial progress using Large Language Models (LLMs) for code generation. Many benchmarks like HumanEval and EvoCodeBench have been created to evaluate LLMs by requiring them to generate code from natural language requirements. However, in enterprise applications and team development, developers typically write code based on structured designs or specifications rather than raw natural language descriptions. This gap between existing benchmarks and real industry development practices means that current benchmark scores may not accurately reflect how much code generation can help automate software development tasks.
  To address this gap, we propose RealBench, a repository-level code generation benchmark aligned with real-world industry software development practices. Each example includes both natural language requirements and UML diagrams as system design, matching how developers typically receive specifications. Based on the constructed benchmarks, we conduct a systematic evaluation of advanced LLMs' code generation capabilities when provided with structured system designs. The experimental results reveal key insights in current LLMs' capabilities for repo-level code generation aligned with real-world software development practices. First, we notice that regarding repo-level code generation, LLMs show much worse performance and there are significant performance gaps among LLMs. Second, LLMs are good at finding and creating modules defined in UML diagrams, but the quality of generated modules is often poor due to grammar and logic errors. Third, generating the entire repository at once is the best generation strategy on smaller repositories, while generating a complex repository with the module-by-module strategy works better compared to other strategies.

</details>


### [79] [Inferring Equivalence Classes from Legacy Undocumented Embedded Binaries for ISO 26262-Compliant Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22673)
*Marco De Luca,Domenico Francesco De Angelis,Domenico Amalfitano,Pasquale Cimmino,Anna Rita Fasolino*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接从编译后的固件二进制码中推断面向输出的等价类划分方法，无需源代码注释或文档，通过控制流重建和引导符号执行分析函数行为，并在工业汽车场景中验证了其正确性与可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业实践中，对遗留无文档的嵌入式固件应用等价类划分测试设计技术时，常因功能规格说明不完整或过时而受阻。ISO 26262等安全标准要求系统性测试，但缺乏文档使得传统方法难以落地。

Method: 提出一种二进制级方法：结合控制流重建与引导符号执行，分析每个函数的执行路径，根据可观察行为（返回值、输出参数）的不可区分性对路径进行分组；可选后处理步骤生成人类可读的表示以支持理解与文档化。

Result: 在工业汽车场景中通过从业者研究评估，结果显示与专家期望高度一致，在可读性和有用性方面获得积极评价，能够有效支持函数理解和测试设计。

Conclusion: 验证了在二进制级别推断等价类划分的可行性和实际相关性，为遗留无文档安全关键嵌入式软件的系统性测试提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Equivalence class partitioning is a well-established test design technique mandated by safety standards such as ISO~26262 for systematic testing of safety software. In industrial practice, however, its application to legacy undocumented embedded firmware is often hindered by incomplete or outdated functional specifications.
  This paper proposes a binary-level methodology for inferring output-oriented equivalence classes directly from compiled firmware, without relying on source-level annotations or external documentation. The approach combines control-flow reconstruction and guided symbolic execution to analyze individual functions and group execution paths according to indistinguishable observable behavior, including return values and output parameters. An optional post-processing step produces human-readable representations to support comprehension and documentation.
  The methodology is evaluated in an industrial automotive context through a practitioner-based study assessing correctness and interpretability. Results indicate strong alignment with expert expectations and a positive perception of readability and usefulness for supporting function understanding and test design. These findings demonstrate the feasibility and practical relevance of binary-level equivalence class inference for systematic testing of legacy undocumented safety-embedded software.

</details>


### [80] [Code for All: Educational Applications of the "Vibe Coding" Hackathon in Programming Education across All Skill Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22747)
*Ashley J. Chen,Yijia Cao,Minghao Shao,Ramesh Karri,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过为期一个月的在线黑客马拉松，研究了使用大型语言模型进行“氛围编码”（仅用AI生成代码，禁止手动编辑）的教育价值，发现不同背景的参与者随着任务复杂度增加展现出不同的参与模式，无手动编辑约束影响了提示词工程和调试实践，这些模式对将AI辅助开发融入编程教育和竞争性学习环境具有启示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索“氛围编码”（自然语言编程）的教育价值，验证其能否在保持有意义学习成果的同时拓宽编程参与面。

Method: 采用混合研究方法，通过为期一个月的在线黑客马拉松（设有三个技术难度递增的赛道），要求参与者仅使用LLM生成代码（禁止手动编辑），结合标准化项目评估（功能性、UI/UX设计、影响力、提示词质量、代码可读性）及赛后问卷调查（感知学习成果、开放式反馈主题分析）。

Result: 揭示了不同背景参与者随任务复杂度增加如何参与氛围编码，无手动编辑约束如何塑造提示词工程和调试实践，以及这些模式对AI辅助编程教育和竞争性学习环境的启示。

Conclusion: 研究发现为将AI辅助开发融入编程教育和竞争性学习环境提供了实证依据和实践启示。

Abstract: The emergence of large language models has enabled vibe coding, a natural language approach to programming in which users describe intent and AI generates or revises code, potentially broadening access to programming while preserving meaningful learning outcomes. We investigate its educational value through a month-long online hackathon that welcomed participants from multiple countries, ranging from complete beginners to experienced developers. The hackathon offered three tracks with increasing technical demands. Spark emphasized basic frontend functionality and dynamic features such as buttons, forms, and API calls. Build required backend or database integration. Launch targeted production ready web applications, including deployment. Participants were required to develop projects using only LLM generated code without manual edits and submitted complete chat histories, source code, demo videos, and functionality reports. We assessed educational effectiveness with a mixed methods design that combined standardized project evaluations across functionality, user interface and user experience design, impact, prompt quality, and code readability, along with post-hackathon surveys of perceived learning outcomes and thematic analysis of open-ended feedback. Our findings describe how participants with different backgrounds engage with vibe coding as task complexity increases, how the no manual editing constraint shapes prompting and debugging practices, and what these patterns imply for integrating AI assisted development into programming education and competitive learning environments.

</details>

### [81] [Implementation and Privacy Guarantees for Scalable Keyword Search on SOLID-based Decentralized Data with Granular Visibility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22100)
*Mohamed Ragab,Faria Ferooz,Mohammad Bahrani,Helen Oliver,Thanassis Tiropanis,Alexandra Poulovassilis,Adriane Chapman,George Roussos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ESPRESSO是一个去中心化搜索框架，支持在Solid个人数据存储(pods)中基于关键词的可扩展搜索，并引入形式化威胁模型分析索引和元数据的安全隐私风险，提出限制元数据暴露和缓解未授权推理的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于Solid架构的去中心化个人数据生态系统中，用户数据存储在各自的pods中，数据分布广泛且受用户特定访问约束，使得跨pods的搜索变得困难。现有方案缺乏有效的隐私保护机制，需要一种既能实现可扩展搜索又能保护用户隐私的解决方案。

Method: 在pods内部构建WebID作用域的索引，采用隐私感知元数据实现跨服务器的源选择和排序；引入形式化威胁模型分析索引和元数据生成、聚合及使用过程中的安全隐私风险。

Result: 识别出意外的元数据泄露和对手推断敏感信息等风险，提出了限制元数据暴露同时缓解未授权推理的关键设计原则。

Conclusion: 提出的威胁模型为评估隐私保护的去中心化搜索提供了基础，为设计具有更强隐私保障的系统提供了指导。

Abstract: In decentralized personal data ecosystems grounded in architectures such as Solid, users retain sovereignty over their data via personal online data stores (pods), hosted on Solid-compliant server infrastructures. In such environments, data remains under the control of pod owners, which complicates search due to distribution across numerous pods and user-specific access constraints. ESPRESSO is a decentralized framework for scalable keyword-based search across distributed Solid pods under user-defined visibility policies. It addresses key challenges of decentralized search by constructing WebID-scoped indexes within pods and employing privacy-aware metadata to enable efficient source selection and ranking across servers. This paper further introduces a formal threat model for ESPRESSO, analysing the security and privacy risks associated with the generation, aggregation, and use of indexes and metadata. These risks include unintended metadata leakage and the potential for adversaries to infer sensitive information about data that resides within personal data stores. The analysis identifies key design principles that limit metadata exposure while mitigating unauthorized inference. The proposed threat model provides a foundation for evaluating privacy-preserving decentralized search and informs the design of systems with stronger privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [82] [MCI: A Maximal Clique Index for Efficient Arbitrary-Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22171)
*Xiaowei Ye,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang,Kaiwen Xue,Daiyin Wang,Xubin Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出基于最大团索引（MCI）的图索引方法，通过最大团覆盖和局部邻域图几何致密化两项关键技术，在高维空间中高效支持带任意过滤谓词的近似最近邻搜索（AFANNS），在10个数据集上相比现有方法实现高达一个数量级的QPS提升，同时占用更少存储空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带过滤谓词的近似最近邻搜索方法存在存储和计算开销大的问题，亟需一种既鲁棒又高效的索引方案。

Method: 提出最大团索引（MCI），包含两项关键技术：(1) 最大团覆盖（MCC），利用高维空间的几何传递性将稠密邻域编码为最大团，实现高压缩比和高连通性；(2) 局部邻域图几何致密化，从稀疏初始NNG开始，通过逐步增大距离阈值进行局部致密化，恢复全局连通性。索引采用无锁并行构建，查询时使用精心设计的多种子策略处理谓词诱导的碎片化子图。

Result: 在10个数据集上，MCI在高召回率下QPS比最先进方法提升高达一个数量级，占用空间显著更小，在范围/关键词过滤任务上也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: MCI是一种鲁棒且高效的通用AFANNS索引，在多种过滤场景下均表现优异。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search with arbitrary filtering predicates (AFANNS) is essential for modern data applications, yet existing methods often incur substantial storage and computational costs. In this work, we introduce the Maximal Clique Index (\mci), a novel graph-based index designed for robust and efficient AFANNS. The core idea of \mci is to approximate a dense Nearest Neighbor Graph (NNG) through a compact, clique-based representation. We propose two key techniques: (1) Maximal Clique Cover (\mcc), which exploits the geometric transitivity of high-dimensional spaces to encode dense neighborhoods as maximal cliques, achieving an index with high compression and connectivity; and (2) Local Neighborhood Graph Geometric Densification, a strategy that constructs an index approximating a large NNG from a sparse initial NNG, recovers global connectivity by progressively increasing distance thresholds to locally densify the structure. The index is built in a lock-free parallel manner for scalability and queried via a carefully-designed multi-seed strategy to handle fragmented predicate-induced subgraphs. Extensive experiments on 10 datasets show that \mci significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to one order of magnitude in QPS at high recall while using substantially smaller space, and remains competitive even on range/keyword filtering tasks, demonstrating robust general-purpose performance.

</details>


### [83] [A Model-Driven Approach to Database Migration with a Unified Data Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22415)
*María J. Ortín,José R. Hoyos,Jesus García-Molina*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于U-Schema统一数据模型的通用数据库迁移方法，通过将各数据模型映射到U-Schema来减少转换次数，支持异构范式间的模式转换和数据迁移，并在关系型到文档型数据库的迁移实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库迁移方法通常针对特定源-目标组合设计，在多模型环境（如关系型与NoSQL系统共存）中适用性有限，缺乏通用的、可跨异构数据模型的迁移方案。

Method: 提出基于U-Schema统一数据模型的中间表示方法，通过定义各数据模型到U-Schema的映射关系，减少所需转换次数；在模式转换过程中生成溯源信息，记录源与目标元素间的对应关系，并以此解耦地指导数据迁移。

Result: 实验表明该方法在往返重构下结构保留度高，生成的文档模式与设计意图一致，在连接、聚合、嵌套结构等多种访问模式下查询行为得以保持，且随数据集规模增长性能可行。

Conclusion: 该方法在设计上支持多数据模型，但当前评估集中于关系型到文档型迁移（含合成数据集与Northwind基准），未来可扩展至更多数据模型。

Abstract: Database migration is a key task in software modernization, increasingly involving transformations across heterogeneous data models such as relational and NoSQL systems. Existing approaches are typically designed for specific source-target combinations, which limits their applicability in multi-model environments.
  This paper proposes a generic database migration approach based on the U-Schema unified data model, which acts as a pivot representation. By defining mappings between each data model and U-Schema, the approach reduces the number of required transformations and enables schema conversion across heterogeneous paradigms. Trace information is generated during schema transformation to capture correspondences between source and target elements, and is subsequently used to guide data migration in a decoupled manner.
  The approach has been implemented and evaluated through experiments covering schema-level validation, data-level semantic preservation, and performance analysis. The results show that the migration pipeline achieves high structural preservation under round-trip reconstruction, produces document schemas consistent with the intended design decisions, and preserves query behavior across a variety of access patterns, including joins, aggregations, and nested structures. Performance results demonstrate the feasibility of the approach for datasets of increasing size.
  The evaluation focuses on relational-to-document migration using both synthetic datasets and the Northwind benchmark. While this scenario provides a concrete instantiation, the approach is designed to support multiple data models within a unified framework.

</details>


### [84] [How Hard is it to Decide if a Fact is Relevant to a Query?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22422)
*Meghyn Bienvenu,Diego Figueira,Pierre Lafourcade*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究数据库查询相关性问题：给定数据库D、布尔合取查询q和事实f，判断f是否属于D中满足q的最小真子集S。研究发现自连接是导致相关性判定比查询评估更困难的关键因素，在禁止或限制自连接后，相关性判定的复杂度可降至与查询评估相同。在DL-Lite_R本体查询场景下，限制交互宽度也能得到类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询相关性是查询答案解释的核心问题，但此前缺乏对其组合复杂度的系统研究，不清楚哪些因素导致了问题的高复杂度，以及哪些限制可以降低复杂度。

Method: 通过复杂度分析，识别自连接（同一关系的多个原子）作为关键因素；证明在禁止或限制自连接时，相关性判定复杂度降至与查询评估相同（NP或LogCFL）；在DL-Lite_R本体查询中引入交互宽度概念并证明类似结果。

Result: (1) 相关性判定对CQ为Σ^p_2-完全，对链式CQ为NP-难；(2) 禁止或限制自连接后，相关性复杂度降至NP（无结构限制）或LogCFL（有界超树宽类）；(3) 在DL-Lite_R本体查询中，限制交互宽度可使相关性复杂度与查询回答复杂度相同。

Conclusion: 自连接是导致相关性判定比查询评估更困难的根本原因，通过限制自连接或交互宽度，可以识别出具有高效相关性计算能力的自然查询类。

Abstract: We consider the following fundamental problem: given a database D, Boolean conjunctive query (CQ) q, and fact f in D, decide whether f is relevant to q wrt. D, i.e., does f belong to a minimal subset S of D such that S |= q. Despite being of central importance to query answer explanation, the combined complexity of deciding query relevance has not been studied in detail, leaving open what makes this problem hard, and which restrictions can yield lower complexity. Relevance has already been shown to be harder than query evaluation: namely, $Σ^p_2$-complete for CQs, even over a binary signature. We further observe that NP-hardness applies already to (acyclic) chain CQs. Our work identifies self-joins (multiple atoms with the same relation) as the culprit. Indeed, we prove that if we forbid or bound the occurrence of self-joins, then relevance has the same complexity as query evaluation, namely, NP (without structural restrictions) and LogCFL (for bounded hypertreewidth classes). In the ontology setting, we establish an analogous result for ontology-mediated queries consisting of a CQ and DL-Lite_R ontology, namely that relevance is no harder than query answering provided that we bound the interaction width (which generalizes both self-join width and a recently introduced 'interaction-free' condition). Our results thus pinpoint what makes relevance harder than query evaluation and identify natural classes of queries which admit efficient relevance computation.

</details>


### [85] [It's Time to Standardize RDF Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22619)
*Pieter Colpaert,Piotr Sowinski*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出RDF消息（RDF Message）概念，将RDF数据集视为原子化通信单元，以解决流式环境中消息边界不明确导致的互操作性问题，并在此基础上定义了RDF消息流、消息日志及消息配置文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RDF语义和工具缺乏对"哪些陈述属于同一消息"的互操作定义，开发者常依赖非标准技术、传输层假设或启发式方法，导致互操作性问题和效率低下。

Method: 提出RDF消息概念（作为原子化通信单元的RDF数据集），明确定义消息边界，并在此基础上构建RDF消息流和RDF消息日志，以及RDF消息配置文件（如Linked Data Event Streams或ActivityStreams）。

Result: 使消息边界在序列化、传输和存储系统中显式化，支持增量消费和可重现回放，适用于物联网观测、归档RDF流、纳米出版物、SPARQL CONSTRUCT结果处理等场景。

Conclusion: 作为W3C RDF流处理社区组的一部分，正在寻求语义Web社区的更广泛支持和反馈。

Abstract: RDF-based systems increasingly operate in event-driven and streaming settings, where producers and consumers exchange data as discrete units of communication rather than as freely mergeable RDF statements. As existing RDF semantics and tooling do not provide an interoperable notion of what statements belong together as one message, developers often rely on out-of-standard techniques, transport-level assumptions, or heuristics, leading to interoperability problems and inefficiencies. We propose the concept of an RDF Message as an RDF Dataset intended to be interpreted atomically as a single communicative act, laying the foundation for defining RDF Message Streams and RDF Message Logs. The proposal makes message boundaries explicit across serializations, transport, and storage systems, which in turn enables incremental consumption and reproducible replay in use cases such as IoT observations, archived RDF streams, nanopublications, or processing SPARQL CONSTRUCT results. Building on this, RDF Message Profiles, such as Linked Data Event Streams or ActivityStreams, then provide the terms for describing pagination, message structure, ordering, or retention policies. As part of the W3C Community Group on RDF Stream Processing, we are now seeking broader support and comments on the proposal from the Semantic Web community.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Structure-Guided Diffusion Model for EEG-Based Visual Cognition Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22649)
*Yongxiang Lian,Yueyang Cang,Pingge Hu,Yuchen He,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构引导扩散模型(SGDM)，通过融合显式结构信息从EEG信号中重建视觉图像，在抽象和自然图像数据集上均优于现有方法，实现了高保真度的视觉解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法局限于自然图像和类别表征，难以捕捉结构特征，且无法区分客观感知与主观认知。作者希望突破这些限制，实现更精细的视觉内容解码。

Method: 采用两阶段生成机制：1) 结构监督变分自编码器 + 时空EEG编码器（通过对比学习对齐到视觉嵌入空间）；2) 通过ControlNet将结构信息注入扩散模型，引导从EEG特征生成图像。

Result: 在抽象物体数据集(Kilogram)和自然图像数据集(THINGS)上均优于现有方法，重建图像在低层视觉特征和语义表示上保真度更高，泛化能力强。EEG时空分析揭示了与视觉认知神经动力学一致的层次化结构编码模式。

Conclusion: SGDM能有效捕捉显式结构几何信息，生成与个体认知表征高度一致的图像，将神经解码扩展到低维或类别输出之外，为高自由度BCI意图解码和更灵活的脑-机通信提供了支持。

Abstract: Objective: Decoding visual information from electroencephalography (EEG) is an important problem in neuroscience and brain-computer interface (BCI) research. Existing methods are largely restricted to natural images and categorical representations, with limited capacity to capture structural features and to differentiate objective perception from subjective cognition. We propose a Structure-Guided Diffusion Model (SGDM) that incorporates explicit structural information for EEG-based visual reconstruction. Approach: SGDM is evaluated on the Kilogram abstract visual object dataset and the THINGS natural image dataset using a two-stage generative mechanism. The framework combines a structurally supervised variational autoencoder with a spatiotemporal EEG encoder aligned to a visual embedding space via contrastive learning. Structural information is integrated into a diffusion model through ControlNet to guide image generation from EEG features. Results: SGDM outperforms existing methods on both abstract and natural image datasets. Reconstructed images achieve higher fidelity in low-level visual features and semantic representations, indicating improved decoding accuracy and strong generalization across diverse visual domains. Spatiotemporal analysis of EEG signals further reveals hierarchical structural encoding patterns, consistent with the neural dynamics of visual cognition. Significance: These findings validate the effectiveness of SGDM in capturing explicit structural geometry and generating images with high fidelity to individual cognitive representations. By enabling decoding of complex visual content from EEG signals, the framework extends neural decoding beyond low-dimensional or categorical outputs. This supports BCIs with increased degrees of freedom for intention decoding and more flexible brain-to-machine communication.

</details>

### [87] [Causality and Semantic Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22041)
*Anna Zhang,Qinglan Luo,London Bielicke,Eunice Jun,Adam Chlipala*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文为科学实验设计的形式化验证提供了语义基础，通过将图论中的d-分离条件与安全领域的非干扰理论联系起来，在Rocq中机械化证明了d-分离与一种新的语义定义完全等价，从而为评估实验设计质量提供了可靠的推理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家在设计实验时常犯关键错误（如忽略混淆变量），而因果推理是科学的基础。虽然Pearl等人已提出因果图形式化框架，但这些思想尚未达到程序语言社区所要求的严格标准——即需要（语法层面的）程序分析被证明相对于自然语义是可靠的。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将d-分离（一种用于判断实验设计是否控制了足够多混淆变量的经典图论条件）与安全理论中的非干扰概念进行语义关联，提出一种新的语义定义，并在Rocq证明助手中机械化验证了二者完全等价。

Result: 证明了d-分离条件与基于非干扰的新语义定义精确一致，为d-分离提供了结构化的语义基础，解释了其正确性而不依赖于概率假设。对于每个实验设计质量的自动化测试，该定理验证了相应的反驳世界建模假设的方法。

Conclusion: 这项工作将因果推理中的核心概念（d-分离）建立在程序语言社区所要求的严格语义基础之上，使其正确性不再依赖于直觉或概率假设，并为科学实验设计的自动化验证提供了理论保障。

Abstract: The design of scientific experiments deserves its own variation of formal verification to catch cases where scientists made important mistakes, such as forgetting to take confounding variables into account. One of the most fundamental underpinnings of science is causality, or what it means for interventions in the world to cause other outcomes, as formalized by computer scientists like Judea Pearl. However, these ideas had not previously been made rigorous to the standards of the programming-languages community, where one expects a (syntactic) program analysis to be proved sound with respect to a natural semantics. In the domain of causality, as the relevant "program analysis," we focus on $d$-separation, a classic condition on graphs that can be used to decide when the design of an experiment controls for sufficiently many confounding variables, even though the reason that this condition works is often unintuitive. Our central result (mechanized in Rocq) is that $d$-separation exactly coincides with a novel semantic definition inspired by noninterference from the theory of security. This characterization provides a structural semantic foundation for $d$-separation and helps explain why the graph-theoretic condition is correct, independently of probabilistic assumptions. For each given automated test on the quality of an experiment design, our theorem justifies an associated method for falsifying the world-modeling hypothesis behind the experiment.

</details>


### [88] [From Monolithic to Compositional: A Compositional Operational Semantics for Crystality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22210)
*Ziyun Xu,Hao Wang,Meng Sun*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文为面向并行EVM的智能合约语言Crystality构建了组合式操作语义，将原有单一语义拆分为引擎组件与全局组件，显式建模并行执行结构。通过局部性、全局隔离性和独立局部步骤的强交换性等关键结构性质的简单证明，以及基于编解码函数的事务级双模拟定理和两级代码级双模拟定理，证明了组合语义与原始语义的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行执行是提升区块链可扩展性的关键途径，但智能合约语言在并行环境下缺乏形式语义，导致严格推理困难。Crystality语言虽支持作用域状态和异步中继，但其原始语义是单一（monolithic）的，无法显式表达并行执行的结构，需要更清晰的组合式语义来支撑形式化推理。

Method: 将原始单一操作语义分解为引擎组件（engine components）和全局组件（global component），构建组合式操作语义；通过编解码函数建立事务级双模拟（bisimulation）定理以及局部与全局两级代码级双模拟定理，证明组合语义与原始语义的等价性。

Result: 组合式语义能够显式表达并行执行结构，并支持局部性（locality）、全局隔离性（global isolation）和独立局部步骤的强交换性（strong commutativity）等关键结构性质的简洁证明；同时通过双模拟定理验证了组合语义与原始语义的语义等价性。

Conclusion: 组合式操作语义为Crystality语言在并行EVM环境下的形式化推理提供了更清晰的基础，其与原始语义的等价性保证了该语义的正确性，为后续在并行区块链场景下对智能合约进行严格推理奠定了基础。

Abstract: Parallel execution has become a key approach to improving blockchain scalability, but the lack of formal semantics for smart contract languages in such settings makes rigorous reasoning difficult. Crystality is a smart contract language designed for parallel EVMs, supporting scoped state and asynchronous relay across execution engines. This paper introduces a compositional operational semantics for Crystality. Unlike the original monolithic semantics, the new semantics decomposes the system into engine components and a global component, making the structure of parallel execution explicit. The compositional formulation enables simple proofs of key structural properties, including locality, global isolation, and strong commutativity of independent local steps. Furthermore, we prove that the compositional semantics is semantically equivalent to the original one via a transaction-level bisimulation theorem based on encoding and decoding functions between configurations, and two code-level bisimulation theorems for local and global execution.

</details>


### [89] [Ownership Refinement Types for Pointer Arithmetic and Nested Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22361)
*Yusuke Fujiwara,Yusuke Matsushita,Kohei Suenaga,Atsushi Igarashi*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Tanaka等人的类型系统，通过泛化所有权概念以引用外部数组索引，支持嵌套数组的验证，并证明了扩展类型系统的可靠性，实现了基于该系统的验证器，成功验证了嵌套数组操作程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tanaka等人的类型系统不支持嵌套数组（如矩阵），无法验证实际数据结构。本文旨在解决该局限，扩展类型系统以支持嵌套数组的正确性验证。

Method: 泛化Tanaka等人的所有权概念，使其能够引用外部数组的索引，从而支持嵌套数组；证明扩展后类型系统的可靠性；实现基于该系统的验证器。

Result: 成功实现了支持嵌套数组的验证器，能够验证Tanaka等人系统无法处理的嵌套数组操作程序的正确性。

Conclusion: 通过扩展所有权概念支持嵌套数组，证明了扩展类型系统的可靠性，实现并验证了其有效性，扩展了程序验证的适用范围。

Abstract: Tanaka et al. proposed a type system for verifying functional correctness properties of programs that use arrays and pointer arithmetic. Their system extends ConSORT -- a type system combining fractional ownership and refinement types for imperative program verification -- with support for pointer arithmetic. Their idea was to extend fractional ownership so that it can depend on an array index. Their formulation, however, does not handle nested arrays, which are essential for representing practical data structures such as matrices. We extend Tanaka et al.'s type system to support nested arrays by generalizing the notion of ownership to be able to refer to the indices of the outer arrays and prove the soundness of the extended type system. We have implemented a verifier based on the proposed type system and demonstrated that it can verify the correctness of programs that manipulate nested arrays, which were beyond the reach of Tanaka et al.

</details>

### [90] [A general optimization solver based on OP-to-MaxSAT reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21961)
*Yuxin Zhao,Han Huang,Zhifeng Hao*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OP-to-MaxSAT的自动化归约方法及基于此的通用优化求解器GORED，能将多种优化问题在多项式时间内归约为MaxSAT实例，并通过单一MaxSAT求解器统一求解。在11类136个实例上的实验表明，GORED能成功求解广泛优化问题，且解的质量与现有方法无统计显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前优化算法大多针对特定问题类型设计，缺乏解决多种优化问题的通用性，因此需要一种统一的自动化归约方法以实现跨类型优化问题的通用求解。

Method: 提出OP-to-MaxSAT归约方法，将多种优化问题在多项式时间内自动归约为MaxSAT实例，并基于此构建通用求解器GORED，利用最先进的MaxSAT求解器统一求解。

Result: 在11类优化问题的136个实例上进行实验验证，GORED能成功求解广泛优化问题，且解的质量与现有方法相比无统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 通过引入自动化归约，该工作将优化求解器的范式从为每种问题类型设计专用算法转变为使用单一算法求解多样问题，使得单一算法的进步能够推动跨领域多种优化问题的进展。

Abstract: Optimization problems are fundamental in diverse fields, such as engineering, economics, and scientific computing. However, current algorithms are mostly designed for specific problem types and exhibit limited generality in solving multiple types of optimization problems. To enhance generality, we propose an automated reduction method named OP-to-MaxSAT reduction and a general optimization solver based on OP-to-MaxSAT reduction (GORED). GORED unifies the solving of multiple types of optimization problems by reducing the problems from optimization problems to MaxSAT instances in polynomial time and solving them using the state-of-the-art MaxSAT solver. The generality and solution quality of GORED are validated through experiments on 136 instances across 11 types of optimization problems. Experimental results demonstrate that GORED not only successfully solves a wide range of optimization problems but also yields solutions comparable in quality to those from existing methods, with no statistically significant differences observed. By introducing automated reduction, this work shifts the paradigm of optimization solvers from designing specialized algorithms for each problem type to employing a single algorithm for diverse problems. As a result, advances in this single algorithm can now drive progress in a wide range of optimization problems across various domains.

</details>


### [91] [Probabilistic Abduction in a Fuzzy Logic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22064)
*Tommaso Flaminio,Katsumi Inoue,Daniil Kozhemiachenko*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在模糊概率逻辑FP中，如何通过概率分布或概率陈述来解释关于事件概率的观察结果，并系统分析了该逻辑下 abduction 问题的解识别与存在性的计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率解释方法缺乏形式化框架来统一处理诸如"下雨概率20%"、"雨雪等概率"等概率观测的解释问题，需要一种逻辑系统来形式化地定义和求解这类 abduction 问题。

Method: 提出模糊概率逻辑FP作为形式化框架，定义 abduction 问题及其解的概念；对完整语言及其析取子句片段进行解识别与存在性的复杂度分析；并将经典概率 abduction（寻找给定事件的最可能解释）转化为FP框架。

Result: 给出了FP中 abduction 问题的解识别与存在性问题的全面复杂度刻画，覆盖了完整语言和析取子句片段；建立了经典概率 abduction 到FP的翻译。

Conclusion: 模糊概率逻辑FP为概率观测的解释提供了形式化基础，其计算复杂度的完整分析为实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study the problem of explaining observations about the probabilities of events, such as "it rains $20\%$ of the time", "rain and snow are equally likely", etc. We explain these statements with a probability distribution or a statement about probabilities of (other) events that are consistent with our knowledge and entail the observation. We formalise this problem in a fuzzy probabilistic logic $\mathsf{FP}$. We define and motivate the notions of abduction problems and their solutions. Our main technical contribution is a comprehensive study of the complexity of solution recognition and existence for a given abduction problem in $\mathsf{FP}$ for the case of full language and its disjunctive-clause fragments. We also obtain a translation of classical probabilistic abduction (finding the most likely explanation of a given event) to $\mathsf{FP}$.

</details>


### [92] [Characterizing LTL Formulas by Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22097)
*Balder ten Cate,Dana Fisman,Roi Ohayon,Patrik Sestic*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性时序逻辑（LTL）公式能否被有限个带标签示例唯一刻画的问题，考察了有限词、超限词和图式示例三种示例类型，并分类了不同LTL片段在各类示例下的唯一可刻画性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同示例类型对LTL公式唯一刻画的表达能力，为示例驱动的规约、调试和时序性质学习提供理论基础。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别考察有限词、超限词和图式示例三类示例；对有限词场景给出了基于限制的LTL片段的完整分类；对超限词场景证明了有限唯一刻画在单调LTL片段上的可行性；引入图式示例作为有限词的紧凑表示以突破仅用有限词的局限。

Result: 1) 在有限词场景下，完成了对限制性LTL片段的唯一可刻画性的完整分类；2) 超限词示例使得大范围单调LTL片段可以实现有限唯一刻画；3) 图式示例在有限场景下实现了仅用普通有限示例无法达成的唯一刻画。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地刻画了不同示例类型对LTL公式唯一刻画的描述能力差异，为示例驱动的时序规约与学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas can be uniquely characterized by a finite set of labeled examples. We consider different types of examples, ranging from finite words to transfinite words, as well as schematic examples. In the finite-word setting, we provide a complete classification of basis-restricted LTL fragments that admit such unique characterizations. Next, we show that allowing transfinite words as examples enables finite unique characterizations for large monotone fragments of LTL. Finally, we introduce schematic examples, i.e., patterns that compactly represent a family of finite words, and we show that these enable unique characterization results in the finite setting that were not possible with ordinary finite examples alone. Overall, the work provides a foundational account of the descriptive power of different example types for example-driven specification, debugging, and learning of temporal properties.

</details>


### [93] [BLAST: Benchmarking LLMs with ASP-based Structured Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22306)
*Manuel Alejandro Borroto Santana,Erica Coppolillo,Francesco Calimeri,Giuseppe Manco,Simona Perri,Francesco Ricca*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLAST，首个专门用于评估大语言模型在Answer Set Programming（ASP）代码生成任务中准确性的基准测试方法及数据集，包含两项新型语义指标，并在十个经典图相关ASP问题和八个先进LLM上进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在自然语言理解、对话系统等领域表现优异，但它们在处理声明式编程范式（如Answer Set Programming）方面的有效性尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出了BLAST基准测试方法，包含结构化评估框架和两种针对ASP代码生成的新型语义指标，并基于十个图相关ASP问题和八个SOTA LLM进行实证评估。

Result: 在十个经典图相关问题及八个先进LLM上完成了实证评估，给出了LLM在ASP代码生成任务中的准确性结果。

Conclusion: BLAST为评估LLM在ASP代码生成方面的准确性提供了首个专用基准，填补了相关研究空白。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across a broad spectrum of tasks, including natural language understanding, dialogue systems, and code generation. Despite evident progress, less attention has been paid to their effectiveness in handling declarative paradigms such as Answer Set Programming (ASP), to date. In this paper we introduce BLAST: The first dedicated benchmarking methodology and associated dataset for evaluating the accuracy of LLMs in generating ASP code. BLAST provides a structured evaluation framework featuring two novel semantic metrics tailored to ASP code generation. The paper presents the results of an empirical evaluation involving ten well-established graph-related problems from the ASP literature and a diverse set of eight state-of-the-art LLMs.

</details>


### [94] [Dynamic Planar Graph Isomorphism is in DynFO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22365)
*Samir Datta,Asif Khan,Felix Tschirbs,Nils Vortmeier,Thomas Zeume*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于经历边插入和删除的平面图，可以用一阶逻辑公式和多项式大小的辅助数据维护两个图是否同构，从而将动态平面图同构问题归入DynFO类，意味着存在一个动态常数时间并行算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索动态图同构问题在描述复杂性中的可维护性边界，特别是针对平面图这一重要图类，研究其是否属于动态描述复杂性类DynFO。

Method: 使用一阶逻辑公式和多项式大小的辅助数据，在动态场景下（边插入和删除操作）维护两个平面图之间的同构关系。

Result: 动态平面图同构问题可以被归入DynFO类，即存在一阶逻辑可维护的动态算法。

Conclusion: 动态平面图同构问题属于DynFO，因此存在动态常数时间并行算法（使用多项式大小辅助数据）来维护两个动态平面图是否同构。

Abstract: Consider two planar graphs which are subject to edge insertions and deletions. We show that whether the two graphs are isomorphic can be maintained with first-order logic formulas and auxiliary data of polynomial size. This places the dynamic planar graph isomorphism problem into the dynamic descriptive complexity class DynFO. As a consequence, there is a dynamic constant-time parallel algorithm with polynomial-size auxiliary data which maintains whether two dynamic planar graphs are isomorphic.

</details>


### [95] [Reelay: Online Temporal Logic Monitoring Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22384)
*Dogan Ulus*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: Reelay 是一个统一的在线时序逻辑监控框架，将多种时序逻辑（LTL、MTL、STL等）转化为可执行的计算图，支持离散/稠密时间语义和delta编码，以C++头文件库+Python接口实现，适用于从嵌入式系统到自主机器人的各类部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息物理系统的时序逻辑监控工具和形式化方法存在碎片化问题，不同的逻辑形式化体系（LTL、MTL、STL等）和工具之间缺乏统一的计算模型和接口，导致在实际应用中难以灵活组合和部署。

Method: 将时序逻辑规约转化为同步数据流系统执行的计算图；支持离散和稠密时间语义；采用delta编码减少带宽占用；实现为header-only C++库并提供Python接口。

Result: 通过案例研究和性能实验验证了框架在实际应用中的可行性，展示了从资源受限嵌入式系统到自主机器人平台等多种部署场景下的高效运行时验证能力。

Conclusion: Reelay成功弥合了表达性形式规约与高效运行时验证之间的差距，为信息物理系统的在线时序逻辑监控提供了一个统一、高效且可广泛部署的框架。

Abstract: We present Reelay, a unified online temporal logic monitoring framework designed for the rigorous analysis and runtime verification of cyber-physical systems. Reelay addresses the fragmentation of existing logical formalisms and tools by providing a single computational model and interface that supports a broad class of temporal logics. These include Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), Metric Temporal Logic (MTL), and Signal Temporal Logic (STL), along with their extensions for robustness semantics and first-order quantification over unbounded categorical data domains. At its core, Reelay translates temporal logic specifications into executable computation graphs operating as synchronous dataflow systems. This architecture ensures an efficient execution mechanism, making the framework ideal for high-frequency data streams regardless of behavior length. Uniquely, the framework supports both discrete and dense-time semantics, as well as delta-encoded temporal behaviors to minimize bandwidth usage in bandwidth-constrained environments. Reelay is implemented as a header-only C++ library with a high-level Python interface, facilitating integration across a wide range of deployment contexts, from resource-constrained embedded systems to autonomous robotic platforms. We demonstrate the practical applicability of the framework through a representative case study and performance experiments, illustrating how Reelay bridges the gap between expressive formal specifications and efficient runtime verification.

</details>


### [96] [Reasoning About Probabilities, Actions, and Knowledge in Fuzzy Modal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22459)
*Daniil Kozhemiachenko,Igor Sedlár*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出了一种模糊模态逻辑，用于形式化关于动作和知识的概率推理，在Kripke框架上定义语义，并分析了不同表达力的片段的可满足性复杂度，发现多个片段可在多项式时间内判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逻辑框架难以同时处理模态算子（知识、动作）与概率推理的交互，需要一个统一的逻辑系统来形式化诸如"执行动作a后，A知道p成立的概率增加/减少/等于0.25"这类包含概率、知识和动作的复合陈述。

Method: 在装备了概率测度的Kripke框架上定义模糊模态逻辑的语义，然后对完整语言及其不同表达力片段的可满足性判定问题进行复杂度分析。

Result: 确定了多个逻辑片段，其可满足性判定问题可在多项式时间内完成。

Conclusion: 该模糊模态逻辑能够有效形式化关于动作和知识的概率推理，且某些受限片段具有计算上的可操作性（多项式时间可判定）。

Abstract: We explore a fuzzy modal logic that can formalise probabilistic reasoning about actions and knowledge. In particular, we deal with contexts involving statements about events expressed via modal formulas, e.g., "after doing $a$, the probability of $A$ knowing that $p$ holds increases / decreases / is equal to $0.25$", "according to $A$, $p$ is equally likely to happen after doing $a$ or $b$", etc. We define the semantics of the logic on Kripke frames equipped with probability measures. We analyse the complexity of deciding the satisfiability of formulas of our logic over finitely branching models, for the full language and its fragments of varying expressivity. In particular, we identify several fragments of our logic where satisfiability is decidable in polynomial time.

</details>


### [97] [On first-order model checking parameterized by the number of variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22493)
*Jan Jedelský*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在图结构上以公式变量数作为参数的一阶模型检测问题的固定参数可解性，刻画了在单调图类中该问题属于FPT的充分必要条件，并在遗传图类中给出了较弱的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知一阶模型检测问题在参数化为公式的量词秩时是AW[*]-难的，而在参数化为变量数时存在XP算法。由于量词秩参数化更为严格，自然的问题是：在哪些图类上，以变量数为参数时该问题可以降为FPT？

Method: 通过刻画单调图类（即对子图封闭的图类）和遗传图类（即对诱导子图封闭的图类）的结构性质，建立以变量数为参数时FO模型检测问题属于FPT的充要条件或近似条件。

Result: 在单调图类情形下给出了完整的刻画（充要条件）；在遗传图类情形下得到了一个稍弱的结果。

Conclusion: 本文填补了以变量数为参数时FO模型检测在受限图类中复杂性的理论空白，区分了量词秩参数化与变量数参数化在结构限制下的不同表现。

Abstract: The first-order (FO) model checking problem asks, given an FO sentence $φ$ and a graph $G$, whether $G$ is a model of $φ$. This problem is known to be $\mathsf{AW[*]}$-hard when parameterized by the quantifier rank of the formula. A classical algorithm decides this problem in XP-time parameterized by the number of variables in the formula.
  Due to $\mathsf{AW[*]}$-hardness, it is natural to ask about the complexity of the problem when restricted to some well-behaved class of graphs. There are many results describing graph classes $\mathcal{C}$ such that the FO model checking problem restricted to $\mathcal{C}$ admits an $\mathsf{FPT}$-time algorithm when parameterized by the quantifier rank of the formula.
  Parameterization by the quantifier rank is significantly more restrictive than parameterization by the number of variables. We investigate the graph classes $\mathcal{C}$ for which the FO model checking problem restricted to $\mathcal{C}$ admits an $\mathsf{FPT}$-time algorithm when parameterized by the number of variables in the formula. We characterize these classes in the monotone setting, and prove a slightly weaker result in the hereditary setting.

</details>


### [98] [The Chase in Lean -- Crafting a Formal Library for Existential Rule Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22531)
*Lukas Gerlach*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文使用Lean形式化验证框架对存在规则（元组生成依赖）及其chase算法进行了建模，证明了chase结果是一个universal model，并在无"alternative matches"时是core，同时统一了MFA等终止条件并支持规则中的常量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: chase算法虽然简单但相关理论研究日益复杂，验证正确性和复现证明变得困难，直觉有时会误导，因此需要形式化验证工具来确保正确性。

Method: 使用Lean编程语言和交互式定理证明器，构建围绕存在规则和chase的形式化框架，讨论chase定义在文献中的细微差别并转化为Lean代码，统一MFA等终止条件并支持常量。

Result: 证明了chase的结果是universal model；无"alternative matches"时是core；将MFA等充分终止条件统一到一个框架中；增加了对规则中常量的支持。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了Lean在知识表示领域的形式化验证能力，为chase算法的正确性和终止性研究提供了可靠的形式化基础。

Abstract: The chase is a sound, complete, but possibly non-terminating algorithm for reasoning with existential rules (aka. tuple-generating dependencies), a highly expressive knowledge representation language. Although the procedure appears simple, research on theoretical properties and optimization for practical implementations has grown to a point where verifying correctness and reproducing proofs becomes challenging and intuition can sometimes be misleading. Lean is a purely functional programming language and interactive theorem prover whose community actively develops formal libraries for mathematics (Mathlib) and computer science (CSLib). In this work, we present our own endeavor of crafting a Lean framework around existential rules and the chase. We discuss design decisions concerning the nuances of chase definitions commonly found in the literature and show how these translate into Lean. To illustrate the framework's capabilities using known results, we show that the result of a chase is a universal model and outline the formalization for proving that without so-called "alternative matches" it is even a core. Beyond existing literature, we unify sufficient chase termination conditions in the likeness of Model-Faithful Acyclicity (MFA) into a common framework while also adding support for constants in rules.

</details>


### [99] [DEKL 2.0: Trace-Indexed Knowledge Evolution in Dependent Type Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22530)
*Chen Peng*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: DEKL 2.0是一个基于依赖类型的迹索引知识演化框架，通过预层语义和迹扩展机制统一了可执行迹、类型化见证与知识修正，并证明了在标准结构规则下证明演算保持单调性而非单调行为源于迹扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识演化框架难以同时处理迹索引、类型化证明和知识修正的统一形式化，缺乏对非单调性在依赖类型理论中的语义刻画。

Method: 将有限迹和无限迹作为计算宇宙的一等对象；知识解释为有限迹范畴上的预层；命题层推理通过范畴论不动点支持处理；建立迹-可达性对应关系及完备性；通过非满射限制映射刻画非单调性；基于转移系统生成的自由范畴给出语义解释。

Result: 证明了证明演算在标准结构规则下保持单调性；非单调行为由迹扩展的语义机制产生；建立了迹-可达性对应关系和完备性结果；用非满射限制映射表征了非单调性的来源。

Conclusion: DEKL 2.0在一个依赖类型语言中统一了可执行迹、类型化见证与知识修正，为迹索引知识演化提供了兼具单调证明论与非单调语义的严谨框架。

Abstract: DEKL 2.0 is a dependent type-theoretic framework for trace-indexed knowledge evolution. Its central claim is that the proof calculus remains monotone under standard structural rules, while non-monotonic behavior arises semantically from trace extension. Finite and infinite traces are first-class objects in the computational universe; knowledge is interpreted as a presheaf over the finite-trace category; and proposition-level reasoning is handled categorically with fixed-point support. We establish trace--reachability correspondence and completeness, characterize non-monotonicity by non-surjective restriction maps, and present a semantic interpretation based on the free category generated by a transition system. The framework unifies executable traces, typed witnesses, and knowledge revision in one dependent language.

</details>


### [100] [An Undecidability Proof for the Plan Existence Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22736)
*Antonis Achilleos*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明了即使在认知行动的前置条件模态深度最多为1且无后置条件的情况下，规划存在问题是不可判定的，该问题此前（不）可判定性未知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究规划存在问题的可判定性边界，特别是认知逻辑中行动前置条件受限情况下的规划问题。

Method: 通过形式化证明，在认知行动的前置条件模态深度≤1且无后置条件的限制下，证明规划存在问题的不可判定性。

Result: 证明了即使在非常受限的条件下（前置条件模态深度≤1，无后置条件），规划存在问题仍然是不可判定的。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了认知规划问题可判定性边界的空白，表明即使是表达力极弱的认知行动类也会导致不可判定性。

Abstract: The plan existence problem asks, given a goal in the form of a formula in modal logic, an initial epistemic state (a pointed Kripke model), and a set of epistemic actions, whether there exists a sequence of actions that can be applied to reach the goal. We prove that even in the case where the preconditions of the epistemic actions have modal depth at most 1, and there are no postconditions, the plan existence problem is undecidable. The (un)decidability of this problem was previously unknown.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Quantifying Interface Procedure Coupling Risks in Digital Nuclear Control Rooms: An Event Based Human Reliability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21932)
*Xingyu Xiao,Mingwei Xiao,Hongbo Li,Jingang Liang,Jiejuan Tong,Haitao Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于2021-2025年核电厂实际运行事件，构建了三维标注框架和四因素界面机制模型，发现界面问题作为风险放大器使程序偏差概率翻倍，语义不匹配和布局诱导陷阱是耦合失效主因，模拟验证确认语义混淆占界面诱发错误的27.3%，提出了数据驱动的人因可靠性分析工作流和界面-程序语义对齐框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化从根本上改变了核主控室的人机交互方式，但界面放大程序风险的定量机制尚不充分明确。

Method: 基于2021-2025年核电厂实际运行事件数据，开发了可复用的三维标注框架和四因素界面机制模型（布局、语义、不匹配、标记缺陷），并结合机器学习解释和模拟器验证。

Result: 42.6%的事件涉及界面缺陷，界面缺陷使程序偏差概率翻倍；语义不匹配和布局诱导陷阱是耦合失效的主因；语义混淆占界面诱发错误的27.3%，错误模式与历史数据一致。

Conclusion: 提出了数据驱动的人因可靠性分析工作流用于数字化控制室早期脆弱性识别，以及界面-程序语义对齐的系统框架以支持风险知情的设计与验证。

Abstract: Digitalization has fundamentally transformed human system interaction in nuclear main control rooms, yet the quantitative mechanisms by which interfaces amplify procedural risks remain insufficiently understood. This study presents a systematic assessment of interface procedure coupling based on real operational events collected from 2021 to 2025 in a modern nuclear power plant. A reusable three dimensional labeling framework and a four factor interface mechanism model are developed to characterize layout, semantic, mismatch, and labeling deficiencies. Results show that interface issues function as a significant risk amplifier. A total of 42.6 percent of events involved interface deficiencies, and their presence more than doubled the likelihood of procedural deviation. Machine learning interpretation further reveals that composite interface procedure coupling, particularly driven by semantic mismatches and layout induced traps, is the dominant contributor to coupled failures. Simulator based validation confirms that semantic confusion accounts for 27.3 percent of interface induced errors, with overall error patterns consistent with historical data. The study provides a data driven HRA workflow for early vulnerability identification in digital control rooms and proposes a systematic framework for interface procedure semantic alignment to support risk informed design and verification.

</details>


### [102] [Not Another EHR: Reimagining Physician Information Needs with Generative AI Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21933)
*Ruican Zhong,Jiachen Li,Gary Hsieh,David W. McDonald,Selin S. Everett,Alyssa Unell,Jonathan Carlson,Katie Claveau,Noel Codella,Khalil Malik,Scott Mackie,Eduardo Olvera,Scott Saponas,Eric Horvitz,David Rhew,Jim Weinstein,Jacob Gross,Amanda K. Hall*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何利用大语言模型（LLMs）构建动态、自适应的生成式用户界面，以减轻电子健康记录（EHRs）给医生带来的认知负担。通过对微软内部医生的半结构化访谈，识别了数据导航与整合中的关键挑战，分析了医生的信息需求及他们对AI辅助工作的心理模型，并提出了以临床医生为中心的工作流设计考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHRs）虽然提高了数据可访问性，但海量复杂数据也增加了医生的认知负担，亟需新的交互方式来改善临床医生的信息处理体验。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，通过对微软内部医生进行半结构化访谈，识别数据导航与整合中的关键挑战，并分析医生对AI辅助工作的心理模型。

Result: 识别了医生在数据导航和整合中的关键挑战，明确了临床诊断工作流中的信息需求特征，并揭示了医生对AI如何辅助工作的心理模型及其对交互和信任期望的影响。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了支持临床医生工作流的生成式用户界面设计考量，为减轻EHR认知负担和优化人机协作提供了方向。

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) have improved data accessibility but have also introduced cognitive burden for physicians, given the sheer volume and complexity of the data involved. Advances in large language models (LLMs) create new opportunities to rethink how clinicians interact with medical data through dynamic, adaptive interfaces. In this position paper, we explore how generative AI can support physicians' information needs by enabling more dynamic interactions with patient data. Through semi-structured interviews with internal physicians at Microsoft, we identify key challenges in data navigation and synthesis, and characterize clinicians' information needs during diagnostic workflows.
